📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

本期重点文献按发表日期、相关性、证据等级和研究质量综合排序,研究结果与综述证据分别核验后形成以下进展。 核心进展:研究结果显示,在异源加强疫苗接种之前有较高的基线中和作用,并且在接受加强疫苗接种后 6 个月内,对 SARS-CoV2 的所有三种变体均具有持久的保护性中和作用。 [10.1093/infdis/jiag363];测序鉴定了所有时间点的 GE.1 谱系。在检测后 23 周之前,检测到 ORF1ab(位置 8942-12458)发生宿主内重组事件,随后病毒载量增加 14 倍(7.42e+06 至 1.00e+08 RNA 拷贝/mL),并且病毒群体发生显着变化。E340D 是一种 sotrovimab 耐药突变,在感染后第一周内以低丰度 (46%) 检测到,随时间波动,并在检测后第 15 周(107 天)几乎固定。虽然所有突变都显示出 E340D 的添加增强了感染性,但这种效果在基线感染性较低的突变中最为明显。 [10.64898/2026.07.14.26356329];在调整年龄、近期感染、暴露环境和时间趋势后,较高的抗体水平,尤其是核衣壳结合抗体和 Omicron 特异性中和抗体,与 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险降低显着相关(调整后的风险比范围为每 log10 倍抗体水平 (AU/mL) 阳性单位差异 0.60 至 0.87)。 [10.1038/s41467-026-74684-8];生物物理分析证实颗粒呈圆形,尺寸约为 180-200 nm。研究发现,免疫小鼠的血清 IgG、IgM 和 IgA 水平高于未感染小鼠。 [10.1186/s12985-026-03256-5];50%的病例出现全身皮疹,而局部皮疹主要影响四肢。 [10.1371/journal.pone.0352201] 本期研究方向以入选文献实际覆盖的临床、流行病学、宿主生态、诊断或分子监测主题为准。 证据边界:部分证据来自摘要、观察性研究或叙述性综述,结论应结合研究设计和证据等级理解。

This literature brief prioritizes papers published in the active reporting window and ranks them by relevance, evidence availability, study quality, recency, and independent-source convergence. Detailed study-specific evidence is retained in the article cards below.

COVID-19 加强疫苗接种和 SARS-CoV-2 突破性感染后的变体特异性和交叉反应性抗体反应

Variant-specific and cross-reactive antibody responses following COVID-19 booster vaccinations and SARS-CoV-2 breakthrough infections
作者： Iris Medits-Weiss, Dominik Moll, David N. Springer, Stephan W. Aberle, Judith H. Aberle, Karin Stiasny
摘要中文翻译摘要 SARS-CoV-2 已进化成多种基因变体,所有变体都带有降低抗体结合并影响疫苗和治疗效果的突变。更新的 COVID-19 疫苗,包括二价制剂(野生型 [WT]/BA.1 或 WT/BA.5)和针对 XBB.1.5、JN.1、KP.2 或 LP.8.1 等新兴变种的最新单价版本,旨在扩大保护范围。然而,免疫印记可能会限制针对显着差异菌株的中和抗体的诱导,即使在接受了几种变体特异性加强剂后也是如此。更深入地了解加强疫苗接种如何重塑抗体特异性对于合理的疫苗设计仍然至关重要。我们检查了抗体对二价 WT/BA.5 加强剂的反应,重点关注抗体水平和中和。将第四剂单价 WT 或二价 (WT/BA.5) mRNA 疫苗前后收集的血清样本与原发 WT 感染和 Omicron BA.1、BA.2 或 BA.5 突破性感染后个体的血清进行比较。我们发现单价和二价加强剂均显着增加了 IgG 和中和抗体,但突破性感染引发了更广泛的交叉反应。耗竭实验表明,加强诱导的免疫主要由交叉反应抗体介导,突破性感染后水平最高,初次 WT 感染后水平最低。这些发现为与变异适应加强疫苗接种后的印记效应相关的抗体特异性提供了功能性见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
更新的 COVID-19 疫苗,包括二价制剂(野生型 [WT]/BA.1 或 WT/BA.5)和针对 XBB.1.5、JN.1、KP.2 或 LP.8.1 等新兴变种的最新单价版本,旨在扩大保护范围。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
将第四剂单价 WT 或二价 (WT/BA.5) mRNA 疫苗前后收集的血清样本与原发 WT 感染和 Omicron BA.1、BA.2 或 BA.5 突破性感染后个体的血清进行比较。
主要结果
我们发现单价和二价加强剂均显着增加了 IgG 和中和抗体,但突破性感染引发了更广泛的交叉反应。
解释与创新
耗竭实验表明,加强诱导的免疫主要由交叉反应抗体介导,突破性感染后水平最高,初次 WT 感染后水平最低。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为与变异适应加强疫苗接种后的印记效应相关的抗体特异性提供了功能性见解。
局限与证据强度
然而,免疫印记可能会限制针对显着差异菌株的中和抗体的诱导,即使在接受了几种变体特异性加强剂后也是如此。

Variant-specific and cross-reactive antibody responses following COVID-19 booster vaccinations and SARS-CoV-2 breakthrough infections

Authors: Iris Medits-Weiss, Dominik Moll, David N. Springer, Stephan W. Aberle, Judith H. Aberle, Karin Stiasny
Original Abstract
Abstract SARS-CoV-2 has evolved into several genetic variants, all bearing mutations that reduce antibody binding and affect vaccine and treatment effectiveness. Updated COVID-19 vaccines, including bivalent formulations (wild type [WT]/BA.1 or WT/BA.5) and more recent monovalent versions targeting emerging variants such as XBB.1.5, JN.1, KP.2 or LP.8.1, were developed to broaden protection. However, immune imprinting may limit the induction of neutralizing antibodies against strains that differ significantly, even after receiving several variant-specific boosters. A deeper understanding of how booster vaccination reshapes antibody specificity remains essential for rational vaccine design. We examined the antibody response to a bivalent WT/BA.5 booster, focusing on antibody levels and neutralization. Serum samples collected before and after a fourth dose of monovalent WT or bivalent (WT/BA.5) mRNA vaccines were compared with sera from individuals after primary WT infections and Omicron BA.1, BA.2, or BA.5 breakthrough infections. We found that both monovalent and bivalent boosters significantly increased IgG and neutralizing antibodies, but breakthrough infections induced broader cross-reactive responses. Depletion experiments revealed that booster-induced immunity was predominantly mediated by cross-reactive antibodies, with the highest levels after breakthrough infections and the lowest after a primary WT infection. These findings provide functional insights into the antibody specificities associated with imprinting effects following variant-adapted booster vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Updated COVID-19 vaccines, including bivalent formulations (wild type [WT]/BA.1 or WT/BA.5) and more recent monovalent versions targeting emerging variants such as XBB.1.5, JN.1, KP.2 or LP.8.1, were developed to broaden protection.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
Serum samples collected before and after a fourth dose of monovalent WT or bivalent (WT/BA.5) mRNA vaccines were compared with sera from individuals after primary WT infections and Omicron BA.1, BA.2, or BA.5 breakthrough infections.
Main results
We found that both monovalent and bivalent boosters significantly increased IgG and neutralizing antibodies, but breakthrough infections induced broader cross-reactive responses.
Interpretation and novelty
Depletion experiments revealed that booster-induced immunity was predominantly mediated by cross-reactive antibodies, with the highest levels after breakthrough infections and the lowest after a primary WT infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide functional insights into the antibody specificities associated with imprinting effects following variant-adapted booster vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, immune imprinting may limit the induction of neutralizing antibodies against strains that differ significantly, even after receiving several variant-specific boosters.

动态参数 SIRD 预测:针对 COVID-19 的可重复时变速率估计

Dynamic-Parameter SIRD Forecasting: ReproducibleTime-Varying Rate Estimation for COVID-19
作者： Juliho David Castillo Colmenares
摘要中文翻译摘要 经典的区室流行病模型通常假设传播、恢复和死亡率恒定,限制了它们跟踪政策转变、行为适应和多波疫情的能力。我们提出了一种混合 SIRD 框架,该框架通过监视序列的直接代数反演来恢复时变转移参数(α(t),β(t),γ(t)),用向量自回归对其联合演化进行建模,并向前集成机械系统。在针对单个全球 COVID-19 聚合系列(2020-2026)的 12 步坚持基准中,根据每日事件计数进行评估,开源 dynasir 实现的新病例 MAPE 为 39.3%,新死亡病例为 29.7%,优于幼稚持久性(两者均为 100%)、静态 SIRD(116.0% 和 172.2%)以及 ARIMA 发生率(631.7%和79.6%)。相对于每个结果的最强非动态参考,这对应于 60.7% 和 62.7% 的减少。估计器保持 O(N) 复杂度并保留可解释的 R0(t) 轨迹。然而,名义上的 95% 预测区间仅实现了 33.3% 的病例覆盖率和 41.7% 的死亡覆盖率。因此,该框架最好被解释为该基准的可重复点预测基线,等待改进的概率校准和更广泛的多区域或跨病原体验证。

问题与背景
相对于每个结果的最强非动态参考,这对应于 60.7% 和 62.7% 的减少。
设计与对象
然而,名义上的 95% 预测区间仅实现了 33.3% 的病例覆盖率和 41.7% 的死亡覆盖率。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
在针对单个全球 COVID-19 聚合系列(2020-2026)的 12 步坚持基准中,根据每日事件计数进行评估,开源 dynasir 实现的新病例 MAPE 为 39.3%,新死亡病例为 29.7%,优于幼稚持久性(两者均为 100%)、静态 SIRD(116.0% 和 172.2%)以及 ARIMA 发生率(631.7%和79.6%)。
解释与创新
估计器保持 O(N) 复杂度并保留可解释的 R0(t) 轨迹。

摘要 经典的区室流行病模型通常假设传播、恢复和死亡率恒定,限制了它们跟踪政策转变、行为适应和多波疫情的能力。
局限与证据强度
因此,该框架最好被解释为该基准的可重复点预测基线,等待改进的概率校准和更广泛的多区域或跨病原体验证。

Dynamic-Parameter SIRD Forecasting: ReproducibleTime-Varying Rate Estimation for COVID-19

Authors: Juliho David Castillo Colmenares
Original Abstract
Abstract Classical compartmental epidemic models typically assume constant transmission, recovery, and mortality rates, limiting their ability to track policy shifts, behavioral adaptation,and multi-wave outbreaks. We present a hybrid SIRD framework that recovers time-varyingtransition parameters (α(t), β(t), γ(t)) by direct algebraic inversion of surveillance series,models their joint evolution with Vector Autoregression, and integrates the mechanistic system forward. In a 12-step held-out benchmark on a single global COVID-19 aggregate series(2020–2026), evaluated on daily incident counts, the open-source dynasir implementationachieved 39.3% MAPE for new cases and 29.7% for new deaths, outperforming naive persistence (100% for both), static SIRD (116.0% and 172.2%), and ARIMA incidence (631.7%and 79.6%). These correspond to 60.7% and 62.7% reductions relative to the strongest nondynamic reference for each outcome. The estimator maintains O(N) complexity and preserves interpretable R0(t) trajectories. However, nominal 95% prediction intervals achievedonly 33.3% coverage for cases and 41.7% for deaths. The framework is therefore best interpreted as a reproducible point-forecasting baseline for this benchmark pending improvedprobabilistic calibration and broader multi-region or cross-pathogen validation.

了解危机中的心理健康:COVID-19 封锁第一周期间比利时心理困扰的关键决定因素

Understanding Mental Health in Crisis: Key Determinants of Psychological Distress in Belgium during the first weeks of COVID-19 Lockdown
作者： Zsabokorszky, Z., Pepermans, K., Van Den Broeck, K., Beutels, P., Hens, N.
摘要中文翻译目标::COVID-19 大流行严重影响了全球心理健康。在大流行爆发时(2020 年),由于疫情爆发和相关的公共卫生措施,与 2018 年相比,比利时人的焦虑、抑郁和心理困扰有所增加。了解这种痛苦的驱动因素对于减轻未来危机中的心理健康影响至关重要。本研究利用大新冠研究 (GCS) 的数据,探讨了 2020 年 3 月封锁期间比利时心理困扰的决定因素。方法:数据来自第二波 GCS,这是一项于 2020 年 3 月 24 日在比利时开展的公民科学倡议,共有 332,169 名受访者。使用一般健康问卷 12 (GHQ-12) 测量心理困扰,并应用 2/3 截止值对困扰级别进行分类。为了确定预测变量,随机森林算法和文献综述将 207 个初始变量减少到 16 个。然后使用广义线性模型来检查预测变量和心理困扰之间的关联。结果:心理困扰与各种人口、社会、职业和健康相关因素显着相关。瓦隆或布鲁塞尔的年轻人、女性和居民表现出更高的痛苦几率。家庭组成和现实生活中社交互动的频率显着影响痛苦程度。职业地位发挥了关键作用,兼职员工和在职学生表现出更高程度的痛苦。与此同时,目前没有职业的退休人员的赔率较低。对工作场所安全的感知和对公共卫生措施的遵守也显着影响了痛苦程度。最后,出现流感样症状或 COVID-19 症状的人出现心理困扰的几率要高得多。结论:我们的研究结果强调了比利时 COVID-19 最初封锁期间心理困扰的重要社会人口、职业和健康相关预测因素。年轻人、女性、面对面互动有限的个人以及患有流感样疾病或 COVID-19 症状的人尤其容易受到影响。此外,对其他人遵守预防措施的看法对心理健康也起着至关重要的作用。这些结果凸显了个人和环境因素在形成心理困扰方面的复杂相互作用,为未来的公共卫生政策和危机期间的心理健康干预措施提供了宝贵的见解。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行采取了前所未有的公共卫生措施,这些措施与心理困扰加剧有关,但在比利时最初的封锁期间,人们对这种困扰的具体决定因素仍知之甚少。该研究旨在确定心理困扰的关键驱动因素,为未来危机环境中的心理健康干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
使用大新冠研究 (GCS) 的数据进行了横断面分析,GCS 是比利时的一项公民科学倡议,于 2020 年 3 月 24 日收集了 332,169 名参与者的反馈。该研究重点关注 2020 年 3 月封锁期间影响心理困扰的社会人口、职业、社交互动和健康相关因素。
核心方法
使用一般健康问卷 12 (GHQ-12) 测量心理困扰,以 2/3 为截止点对困扰级别进行分类。随机森林算法和文献综述将 207 个初始变量减少为 16 个预测变量,然后使用广义线性模型进行分析,以检查与心理困扰的关联。
主要结果
心理困扰与年龄较小、女性、居住在瓦隆或布鲁塞尔、家庭组成、现实生活中社交互动频率较低、兼职工作或学生身份、缺乏当前职业、感知工作场所安全、遵守公共卫生措施以及经历流感样或 COVID-19 症状显着相关。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明封锁期间的心理困扰是由个人脆弱性和环境因素复杂的相互作用造成的,强调了特别脆弱的群体,如年轻人、女性以及社交互动或症状有限的人。新颖之处在于大规模的公民科学方法以及将感知遵守预防措施确定为心理健康的决定因素。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以通过识别高风险群体和可改变的因素(例如未来危机期间的社交互动频率和工作场所安全认知)来为有针对性的心理健康干预措施和公共卫生政策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
横截面设计限制了因果推断,并且对自我报告数据的依赖可能会引入偏差。尽管样本量很大,但对其他背景或人群的普遍性仍然不确定,需要对研究结果进行谨慎解释。

Understanding Mental Health in Crisis: Key Determinants of Psychological Distress in Belgium during the first weeks of COVID-19 Lockdown

Authors: Zsabokorszky, Z., Pepermans, K., Van Den Broeck, K., Beutels, P., Hens, N.
Original Abstract
Aims: : The COVID-19 pandemic has significantly impacted global mental health. At the onset of the pandemic (2020), Belgians experienced increased anxiety, depression, and psychological distress compared to 2018 due to the outbreak and the associated public health measures. Understanding the drivers of this distress is crucial for mitigating mental health effects in future crises. This study examines determinants of psychological distress in Belgium during the March 2020 lockdown, using data from the Great Corona Study (GCS). Methods: Data were drawn from the second wave of the GCS, a citizen science initiative conducted in Belgium on March 24, 2020, with 332,169 respondents. Psychological distress was measured using the General Health Questionnaire-12 (GHQ-12), applying a 2/3 cutoff to classify distress levels. To identify predictor variables, a random forest algorithm and literature review reduced 207 initial variables to 16. A generalized linear model was then used to examine associations between predictors and psychological distress Results: Psychological distress was significantly associated with various demographic, social, occupational, and health-related factors. Younger individuals, women, and residents of Wallonia or Brussels exhibited higher odds of distress. Household composition, and the frequency of real-life social interactions significantly influenced distress levels. Occupational status played a key role, with part-time employees and working students exhibiting higher levels of distress. At the same time retired individuals with no current occupation showed lower odds. Perceived workplace safety and compliance with public health measures also significantly impacted distress levels. Lastly, individuals experiencing influenza-like or COVID-19 symptoms had substantially higher odds of psychological distress. Conclusions: Our findings highlight significant sociodemographic, occupational, and health-related predictors of psychological distress during the initial COVID-19 lockdown in Belgium. Young adults, women, individuals with limited in-person interactions, and those experiencing influenza-like illness or COVID-19 symptoms were particularly vulnerable. Additionally, perceptions of others' adherence to preventive measures played a crucial role in mental well-being. These results highlight the complex interplay between individual and environmental factors in shaping psychological distress, providing valuable insights for future public health policies and mental health interventions during crises.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The COVID-19 pandemic imposed unprecedented public health measures that were associated with increased psychological distress, yet the specific determinants of this distress remained insufficiently understood in Belgium during the initial lockdown. The study aimed to identify key drivers of psychological distress to inform future mental health interventions in crisis settings.
Design and population
A cross-sectional analysis was conducted using data from the Great Corona Study (GCS), a citizen science initiative in Belgium that collected responses on March 24, 2020, from 332,169 participants. The study focused on sociodemographic, occupational, social interaction, and health-related factors influencing psychological distress during the March 2020 lockdown.
Core methods
Psychological distress was measured using the General Health Questionnaire-12 (GHQ-12) with a 2/3 cutoff to classify distress levels. A random forest algorithm and literature review reduced 207 initial variables to 16 predictor variables, which were then analyzed using a generalized linear model to examine associations with psychological distress.
Main results
Psychological distress was significantly associated with younger age, female gender, residence in Wallonia or Brussels, household composition, lower frequency of real-life social interactions, part-time employment or student status, lack of current occupation, perceived workplace safety, compliance with public health measures, and experiencing influenza-like or COVID-19 symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that psychological distress during the lockdown was shaped by a complex interplay of individual vulnerabilities and environmental factors, highlighting particularly vulnerable groups such as young adults, women, and those with limited social interactions or symptoms. The novelty lies in the large-scale citizen science approach and the identification of perceived adherence to preventive measures as a determinant of mental well-being.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform targeted mental health interventions and public health policies by identifying high-risk groups and modifiable factors such as social interaction frequency and workplace safety perceptions during future crises.
Limitations and evidence strength
The cross-sectional design limits causal inference, and the reliance on self-reported data may introduce bias. Despite the large sample size, the generalizability to other contexts or populations remains uncertain, warranting cautious interpretation of the findings.

斐济卫生系统工作人员在异源加强疫苗接种前后针对 SARS-CoV-2 祖先、Delta 和 Omicron 变种的中和能力

Neutralisation capacity against SARS-CoV-2 ancestral, Delta and Omicron variants before and after heterologous booster vaccination in Fijian health system workers
作者： Anaseini Ratu, Isireli Rabukawaqa, Reuben McGregor, Josephine Sassen, Naina Cagi, Kitione Veitogavi, Venina Naigulevu, Reapi Seru, Luse Lauci, Ciara Ramiah
摘要中文翻译摘要 对太平洋岛国的 COVID-19 疫苗加强剂量的免疫原性研究有限。在斐济的医护人员中进行了一项前瞻性队列研究,以评估在主要剂量方案给药期间,在 Delta 感染强度较高的情况下,针对祖先、Delta 和 Omicron (BA.2) 变种的抗体中和能力。研究结果显示,在异源加强疫苗接种之前有较高的基线中和作用,并且在接受加强疫苗接种后 6 个月内,对 SARS-CoV2 的所有三种变体均具有持久的保护性中和作用。该人群从第三剂疫苗中获得的益处有限,这凸显了需要根据当地疾病情况制定务实的疫苗接种策略。

Neutralisation capacity against SARS-CoV-2 ancestral, Delta and Omicron variants before and after heterologous booster vaccination in Fijian health system workers

Authors: Anaseini Ratu, Isireli Rabukawaqa, Reuben McGregor, Josephine Sassen, Naina Cagi, Kitione Veitogavi, Venina Naigulevu, Reapi Seru, Luse Lauci, Ciara Ramiah
Original Abstract
Abstract Immunogenicity studies of booster doses of COVID-19 vaccines from Pacific Island countries are limited. A prospective cohort study was conducted amongst healthcare workers in Fiji to assess antibody neutralisation capacity against ancestral, Delta and Omicron (BA.2) variants following high force of infection from Delta during administration of primary dose schedules. Findings showed high baseline neutralisation prior to heterologous booster vaccinations and enduring protective neutralisation against all three variants of SARS-CoV2 at 6 months following booster receipt. Limited benefit from third doses in this population emphasises the need for pragmatic vaccination strategies guided by local disease contexts.

Main results
Findings showed high baseline neutralisation prior to heterologous booster vaccinations and enduring protective neutralisation against all three variants of SARS-CoV2 at 6 months following booster receipt.
Interpretation and novelty
The supplied evidence does not clearly distinguish the authors' interpretation or the study's novelty.

Limited benefit from third doses in this population emphasises the need for pragmatic vaccination strategies guided by local disease contexts.

单克隆抗体暴露前预防后宿主内 SARS-CoV-2 的复杂进化

Complex intra-host SARS-CoV-2 evolution following monoclonal antibody pre-exposure prophylaxis
作者： Kimia Kamelian, David J. Pascall, Mark Tsz Kin Cheng, Meng, B., Mazharul Altaf, Rebecca M Morse, Juliana Bernardi Aggio, Daniel Egan, Michael Chen-Xu, Giorgio Trivioli
摘要中文翻译背景单克隆抗体已成为预防免疫功能低下个体出现症状的 SARS-CoV-2 感染的预防策略。然而,这种机制下突破性感染的进化和临床意义仍不清楚。方法 一名患有血液学/肿瘤学诊断的 80 多岁男性于 2023 年 3 月接受了 2000 mg 静脉输注 sotrovimab,并于 2023 年 8 月通过鼻咽拭子 RT-qPCR 诊断为 COVID-19。截至 2024 年 2 月(171 天),每周收集样本(n = 24)。所有样本均经过全基因组测序,并对选定的突变进行功能评估。结果测序在所有时间点均鉴定出 GE.1 谱系。在检测后 23 周之前,检测到 ORF1ab(位置 8942-12458)发生宿主内重组事件,随后病毒载量增加 14 倍(7.42e+06 至 1.00e+08 RNA 拷贝/mL),并且病毒群体发生显着变化。E340D 是一种 sotrovimab 耐药突变,在感染后第一周内以低丰度 (46%) 检测到,随时间波动,并在检测后第 15 周(107 天)几乎固定。我们评估了五个刺突突变——N 端结构域中的 V36M、S98F 和 V213G,受体结合结构域中的 Y505P,以及 S1/S2 切割位点附近的 P681Q——并另外评估了 E340D 的影响。V36M 在所有细胞系中赋予最高的感染性,在低 TMPRSS2 细胞中效果最显着。虽然所有突变都显示出 E340D 的添加增强了感染性,但这种效果在基线感染性较低的突变中最为明显。添加 E340D 显着降低了 V36M、S98F 和 V213G 的相对中和滴度,使得 XBB 反应个体能够逃脱中和抗体,说明表型优势增强。在使用 sotrovimab 之前患者不存在中和活性,并且选择 E340D 进一步损害了 sotrovimab 诱导的中和活性。解释 Sotrovimab 在免疫功能低下患者中进行暴露前预防并不能预防 SARS-CoV-2 感染,并且选择了耐药突变 E340D,在非受体结合域尖峰区域产生了意想不到的适应性后果。资助Wellcome Grant (220540/Z/20/A)、Wellcome Sanger Institute 五年回顾 2021 香港赛马会全球健康研究所 NIHR 剑桥生物医学研究中心 (NIHR203312) 盖茨剑桥信托哈丁杰出研究生学者计划 Glaxosmithkline (GSK) Xunta de Galicia ED481A-2023/089
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
单克隆抗体已成为预防免疫功能低下个体出现症状的 SARS-CoV-2 感染的预防策略,但这种机制下突破性感染的进化和临床意义仍不清楚。
设计与对象
一名患有血液学/肿瘤学诊断的 80 多岁男性于 2023 年 3 月接受了 2000 mg 静脉输注 sotrovimab,并于 2023 年 8 月通过鼻咽拭子 RT-qPCR 诊断为 COVID-19。截至 2024 年 2 月(171 天),每周收集样本(n = 24)。
核心方法
所有样本均经过全基因组测序,并对选定的突变进行功能评估。我们评估了五个刺突突变——N 端结构域中的 V36M、S98F 和 V213G,受体结合结构域中的 Y505P,以及 S1/S2 切割位点附近的 P681Q——并另外评估了 E340D 的影响。
主要结果
测序鉴定了所有时间点的 GE.1 谱系。在检测后 23 周之前,检测到 ORF1ab(位置 8942-12458)发生宿主内重组事件,随后病毒载量增加 14 倍(7.42e+06 至 1.00e+08 RNA 拷贝/mL),并且病毒群体发生显着变化。E340D 是一种 sotrovimab 耐药突变,在感染后第一周内以低丰度 (46%) 检测到,随时间波动,并在检测后第 15 周(107 天)几乎固定。虽然所有突变都显示出 E340D 的添加增强了感染性,但这种效果在基线感染性较低的突变中最为明显。
解释与创新
免疫功能低下患者的 Sotrovimab 暴露前预防并不能预防 SARS-CoV-2 感染,并选择了耐药突变 E340D,在非受体结合域尖峰区域产生了意想不到的适应性后果。
科研与公卫意义
E340D 的选择及其对病毒适应性和抗体逃逸的影响凸显了单克隆抗体预防驱动病毒进化和降低中和抗体功效的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个患者病例,这限制了研究结果的普遍性。由于详细的纵向采样和功能评估,证据强度中等,但单患者设计引入了潜在的混杂和偏差。

Complex intra-host SARS-CoV-2 evolution following monoclonal antibody pre-exposure prophylaxis

Authors: Kimia Kamelian, David J. Pascall, Mark Tsz Kin Cheng, Meng, B., Mazharul Altaf, Rebecca M Morse, Juliana Bernardi Aggio, Daniel Egan, Michael Chen-Xu, Giorgio Trivioli
Original Abstract
BackgroundMonoclonal antibodies have emerged as a prophylactic strategy to prevent symptomatic SARS-CoV-2 infection in immunocompromised individuals. However, the evolutionary and clinical implications of breakthrough infections under this regime remain unclear. MethodsA male in their 80s with a haematological/oncological diagnosis received a 2000 mg intravenous infusion of sotrovimab in March 2023 and was diagnosed with COVID-19 by RT-qPCR from a nasopharyngeal swab in August 2023. Weekly samples (n=24) were collected through February 2024 (171 days). All samples underwent whole-genome sequencing, with select mutations subjected to functional assessment. FindingsSequencing identified the GE.1 lineage at all timepoints. An intra-host recombination event in ORF1ab (positions 8942-12458) was detected prior to 23 weeks post-detection, followed by a 14-fold increase in viral load (7.42e+06 to 1.00e+08 RNA copies/mL) and a marked shift in the viral population. E340D, a sotrovimab resistance mutation, was detected at low abundance (46%) within the first week post-infection, fluctuated over time, and was nearly fixed by week 15 (107 days) post-detection. We assessed five spike mutations - V36M, S98F, and V213G in the N-terminal domain, Y505P in the receptor-binding domain, and P681Q near the S1/S2 cleavage site- and additionally evaluated the impact of E340D. V36M conferred the highest infectivity across all cell lines, with the most significant effect in low-TMPRSS2 cells. While all mutations showed enhanced infectivity with the addition of E340D, the effect was most pronounced in mutations with lower baseline infectivity. The addition of E340D significantly decreased relative neutralizing titres for V36M, S98F, and V213G, enabling escape from neutralizing antibodies in XBB-responsive individuals, illustrating an enhanced phenotypic advantage. Patient neutralizing activity was absent pre-sotrovimab, and sotrovimab-induced neutralization was further compromised by selection of E340D. InterpretationSotrovimab pre-exposure prophylaxis in an immunocompromised patient did not prevent SARS-CoV-2 infection, and selected for resistant mutation E340D, with unexpected fitness consequences across non-receptor binding domain spike regions. FundingWellcome Grant (220540/Z/20/A), Wellcome Sanger Institute Quinquennial Review 2021 Hong Kong Jockey Club Global Health Institute NIHR Cambridge Biomedical Research Centre (NIHR203312) Gates Cambridge Trust Harding Distinguished Postgraduate Scholars Programme Glaxosmithkline (GSK) Xunta de Galicia ED481A-2023/089
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Monoclonal antibodies have emerged as a prophylactic strategy to prevent symptomatic SARS-CoV-2 infection in immunocompromised individuals, but the evolutionary and clinical implications of breakthrough infections under this regime remain unclear.
Design and population
A male in their 80s with a haematological/oncological diagnosis received a 2000 mg intravenous infusion of sotrovimab in March 2023 and was diagnosed with COVID-19 by RT-qPCR from a nasopharyngeal swab in August 2023. Weekly samples (n=24) were collected through February 2024 (171 days).
Core methods
All samples underwent whole-genome sequencing, with select mutations subjected to functional assessment. We assessed five spike mutations - V36M, S98F, and V213G in the N-terminal domain, Y505P in the receptor-binding domain, and P681Q near the S1/S2 cleavage site- and additionally evaluated the impact of E340D.
Main results
Sequencing identified the GE.1 lineage at all timepoints. An intra-host recombination event in ORF1ab (positions 8942-12458) was detected prior to 23 weeks post-detection, followed by a 14-fold increase in viral load (7.42e+06 to 1.00e+08 RNA copies/mL) and a marked shift in the viral population. E340D, a sotrovimab resistance mutation, was detected at low abundance (46%) within the first week post-infection, fluctuated over time, and was nearly fixed by week 15 (107 days) post-detection. While all mutations showed enhanced infectivity with the addition of E340D, the effect was most pronounced in mutations with lower baseline infectivity.
Interpretation and novelty
Sotrovimab pre-exposure prophylaxis in an immunocompromised patient did not prevent SARS-CoV-2 infection, and selected for resistant mutation E340D, with unexpected fitness consequences across non-receptor binding domain spike regions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The selection of E340D and its impact on viral fitness and antibody escape highlights the potential for monoclonal antibody prophylaxis to drive viral evolution and reduce the efficacy of neutralizing antibodies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single patient case, which limits the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the detailed longitudinal sampling and functional assessment, but the single-patient design introduces potential confounding and bias.

COVID-19 疫苗异源与同源加强的系统免疫分析

Systemic immune profiling of heterologous versus homologous boosting of COVID-19 vaccination
作者： Xu Han, Hudachuan Jiang, Hui Zheng, Pengfei Jin, Lingjie Xu, Fengze Wang, Simin Li, Chengwei Shao, 贾斯月, Jialu Feng
摘要中文翻译背景:与同源加强相比,初免后使用不同的 COVID-19 疫苗进行异源加强可产生更强的针对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (SARS-CoV-2) 及其变体的抗体反应,特别是对于灭活的 COVID-19 疫苗 (CoronaVac)。然而,目前尚不清楚异源增强潜在的免疫增强机制。方法:在本研究中,我们使用单 B 细胞分选平台,在使用 CoronaVac 进行同源加强接种或使用基于两剂 CoronaVac 的异源加强接种 Ad5-nCoV 后第 180 天分离出刺突蛋白结合特异性单克隆抗体。随后,我们验证了它们对 SARS-CoV-2 变体的中和活性、种系基因序列以及针对 SARS-CoV-2 NTD/RBD/S1 的亲和动力学。此外,我们通过整合单细胞RNA/V(D)J测序(scRNA/V(D)J-seq)对免疫反应特征进行了深入分析。结果:我们的研究表明,与 CoronaVac 的同源加强相比,Ad5-nCoV 的异源加强能引发更多具有更高亲和力的成熟 B 细胞,并激活更丰富的免疫相关途径。此外,Ad5-nCoV 加强版扩增了 B 细胞和 T 细胞的独特克隆类型,而 CoronaVac 加强版则导致了小规模的克隆扩增。此外,与中和抗体相关的种系的利用在 Ad5-nCoV 加强的受体中优先富集。结论:最重要的是,我们的研究为通过新型单 B 细胞分选平台和 scRNA/V(D)J-seq 技术阐述异源增强 COVID-19 免疫的系统免疫景观提供了见解。试用注册号:NCT04892459。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究调查了异源加强与同源加强相比的免疫增强机制,特别关注 CoronaVac 引发和 Ad5-nCoV 或 CoronaVac 加强后免疫反应的潜在差异。
设计与对象
该研究使用单一 B 细胞分选平台在加强后第 180 天从参与者中分离出刺突蛋白结合特异性单克隆抗体,并随后验证了它们的中和活性和亲和动力学。
核心方法
研究人员整合单细胞RNA/V(D)J测序(scRNA/V(D)J-seq)对免疫反应特征进行深入分析,包括种系基因序列和通路。
主要结果
与 CoronaVac 同源加强相比,Ad5-nCoV 异源加强引发了更成熟的 B 细胞,具有更高的亲和力,并激活了更丰富的免疫相关途径,并扩展了 B 和 T 细胞的独特克隆类型。
解释与创新
该研究为异源增强 COVID-19 免疫的系统免疫景观提供了新的见解,强调了与 Ad5-nCoV 增强剂中中和抗体相关的种系优先富集。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对异源加强背后的免疫机制的更深入的了解,这可以为未来的疫苗策略和加强方案的设计提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个时间点(第 180 天),并依赖于单个 B 细胞分选平台和 scRNA/V(D)J-seq 技术,该技术可能无法捕获免疫反应的完整动态。由于关注机制见解而不是广泛的临床结果,证据强度中等。

Systemic immune profiling of heterologous versus homologous boosting of COVID-19 vaccination

Authors: Xu Han, Hudachuan Jiang, Hui Zheng, Pengfei Jin, Lingjie Xu, Fengze Wang, Simin Li, Chengwei Shao, 贾斯月, Jialu Feng
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Compared with homologous boosting, heterologous boosting with a different COVID-19 vaccine following priming generates stronger antibody responses against severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) as well as variants, particularly for inactivated COVID-19 vaccine(CoronaVac). However, it is still unclear about the potential immune enhancement mechanism underlying heterologous boosting. METHODS: In this study, we isolated spike protein binding-specific monoclonal antibodies at day 180 post a homologous booster with CoronaVac or a heterologous booster with Ad5-nCoV based on two-dose of CoronaVac using the single B cell sorting platform. Subsequently, we verified their neutralization activity to SARS-CoV-2 variants, germline gene sequences and affinity kinetics targeting SARS-CoV-2 NTD/RBD/S1. Additionally, we conducted an in-depth analysis of the immunological response characteristics, by integrating single-cell RNA/V(D)J sequencing(scRNA/ V(D)J-seq). RESULTS: Our study demonstrated that heterologous boosting with Ad5-nCoV elicited more mature B cells with higher affinity and activated more abundant immune-related pathways compared to the homologous boosting with CoronaVac. In addition, Ad5-nCoV boosting expanded unique clonal types of B and T cells, whereas CoronaVac boosting led to a small-sized clonal expansion. Furthermore, the utilization of germlines associated with neutralizing antibody were preferentially enriched in recipients with Ad5-nCoV boosting. CONCLUSIONS: Above all, our study gives insights for elaborating the systemic immune landscape of heterologous-boosting COVID-19 immunization by the novel single B cell sorting platform and scRNA/V(D)J-seq technology. TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT04892459.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study investigates the immune enhancement mechanism of heterologous boosting compared to homologous boosting, specifically focusing on the potential differences in immune responses following priming with CoronaVac and boosting with Ad5-nCoV or CoronaVac.
Design and population
The study isolated spike protein binding-specific monoclonal antibodies from participants at day 180 post-booster using a single B cell sorting platform, and subsequently verified their neutralization activity and affinity kinetics.
Core methods
The researchers integrated single-cell RNA/V(D)J sequencing (scRNA/V(D)J-seq) to conduct an in-depth analysis of the immunological response characteristics, including germline gene sequences and pathways.
Main results
Heterologous boosting with Ad5-nCoV elicited more mature B cells with higher affinity and activated more abundant immune-related pathways compared to homologous boosting with CoronaVac, and expanded unique clonal types of B and T cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provides novel insights into the systemic immune landscape of heterologous-boosting COVID-19 immunization, highlighting the preferential enrichment of germlines associated with neutralizing antibodies in Ad5-nCoV boosters.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings offer a deeper understanding of the immune mechanisms behind heterologous boosting, which could inform future vaccine strategies and the design of booster regimens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single time point (day 180) and relies on a single B cell sorting platform and scRNA/V(D)J-seq technology, which may not capture the full dynamics of the immune response. The evidence strength is moderate due to the focus on mechanistic insights rather than broad clinical outcomes.

SARS-CoV-2膜蛋白病毒样颗粒的生成和免疫学评价

Generation and immunological evaluation of SARS-CoV-2 membrane protein virus-like particles
作者： Akash Kumar, Krishna Kishore Inampudi, Vikas Kumar, Rajan Singh, Ghanshyam P. Sinha, Mohammad K. Parvez, Deepak Sehgal
摘要中文翻译背景:SARS-CoV-2大流行的出现导致了高传染性变种的传播,例如起源于印度的Delta变种,这凸显了开发新的抗病毒药物、治疗方法和疫苗的迫切需要。在我们之前的研究中,我们表明膜包膜病毒样颗粒表现出抗原性和中和活性。因此,我们目前的研究旨在评估单独的 M 蛋白是否可以形成引发免疫反应的 VLP。方法:使用计算方法,我们确定了 M 蛋白中有助于 VLP 形成并与其他结构蛋白相互作用的关键相互作用残基,包括刺突 (S)、核衣壳 (N) 和包膜 (E)。SARS-CoV-2-M 蛋白在 Sf-21 昆虫细胞中表达,所得的 VLP 经纯化、形状和大小分析,并使用 DLS、FESEM 和 TEM 进行表征。将纯化的 VLP 注射到 BALB/c 小鼠中,以与未感染的对照小鼠相比评估其免疫反应。结果:生物物理分析证实颗粒呈圆形,尺寸约为 180-200 nm。研究发现,免疫小鼠的血清 IgG、IgM 和 IgA 水平高于未感染小鼠。进一步的 qRT-PCR 分析显示了 IFN-γ、IL-2 和 IL-12 的水平,表明针对 M 蛋白的 TH1 偏向免疫反应。结论:我们的研究表明,高度保守的 M 蛋白可以自组装成 VLP,并引发体液和细胞免疫反应。此外,我们的研究表明,虽然 M 蛋白 VLP 引发显着的抗体和细胞因子反应,但单独给予时,它们不会诱导可检测的中和活性。

SARS-CoV-2-M 蛋白在 Sf-21 昆虫细胞中表达,所得的 VLP 经纯化、形状和大小分析,并使用 DLS、FESEM 和 TEM 进行表征。将纯化的 VLP 注射到 BALB/c 小鼠中,以与未感染的对照小鼠相比评估其免疫反应。

生物物理分析证实颗粒呈圆形,尺寸约为 180-200 nm。研究发现,免疫小鼠的血清 IgG、IgM 和 IgA 水平高于未感染小鼠。

科研与公卫意义
该研究表明 M 蛋白可以形成 VLP 并引发强大的免疫反应,为疫苗开发提供了一种新方法,这可能是包含在多表位疫苗中的候选者。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于使用单一小鼠模型且缺乏与其他疫苗平台的直接比较而受到限制。由于研究结果的初步性质,证据强度中等。

Generation and immunological evaluation of SARS-CoV-2 membrane protein virus-like particles

Authors: Akash Kumar, Krishna Kishore Inampudi, Vikas Kumar, Rajan Singh, Ghanshyam P. Sinha, Mohammad K. Parvez, Deepak Sehgal
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: The emergence of the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic led to the spread of highly transmissible variants, such as the Delta variant, which originated in India, underscoring the urgent need to develop new antivirals, therapeutics, and vaccines. In our previous study, we showed that Membrane-Envelope Virus-like Particles exhibit antigenicity and neutralization activity. Hence, our present study was conducted to evaluate whether the M protein alone can form VLPs that elicit an immune response. METHODS: Using computational methods, we identified key interacting residues in M-protein that contribute to VLP formation and interact with other structural proteins, including Spike (S), Nucleocapsid (N), and Envelope (E). The SARS-CoV-2-M protein was expressed in Sf-21 insect cells, and the resulting VLPs were purified, analyzed for shape and size, and characterized using DLS, FESEM, and TEM. The purified VLPs were injected into BALB/c mice to evaluate their immune response compared with uninfected controls. RESULTS: The biophysical analysis confirms that the particles are round and have a size of ~ 180-200 nm. The serum levels of IgG, IgM, and IgA were found to be higher in immunized mice than in uninfected mice. Further qRT-PCR analysis demonstrated the levels of IFN-γ, IL-2, and IL-12, indicating a TH1-biased immune response against the M protein. CONCLUSION: Our study demonstrates that the highly conserved M protein can self-assemble into VLPs and elicit humoral and cellular immune response. Furthermore, our study indicates that while M-protein VLPs elicit significant antibodies and cytokine responses, they do not induce detectable neutralizing activity when given alone.
View research seven-element analysis

Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a novel approach for vaccine development by showing that the M protein can form VLPs and elicit a robust immune response, which could be a candidate for inclusion in multi-epitope vaccines.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single mouse model and the absence of a direct comparison with other vaccine platforms. The evidence strength is moderate due to the preliminary nature of the findings.

omicron 变体原发性 COVID-19 感染的皮肤表现

Skin manifestations of primary COVID-19 infection with the omicron variant
作者： Chuan Ma, Jiachen Sun, Zilian Liu, Chunlei Zhang, Yuansheng Chen, Jiawang Ma, Qing Ma
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管越来越多的早期变种记录了 COVID-19 的皮肤表现,但与 Omicron 变种特别相关的皮肤病学特征,特别是在中国人群中,在文献中仍然没有充分描述。目的:描述由 Omicron 变种引起的原发性 COVID-19 感染患者的皮肤表现及相应的治疗。方法:本次回顾性分析纳入了 94 名参与者,他们在感染 COVID-19 Omicron 变体后 1 个月内出现皮肤症状。结果:平均年龄为 38 岁,范围为 7 岁至 89 岁;其中66%的人年龄在25-50岁之间。据报道,女性皮肤症状的发生率明显较高(男性与女性,1:2.76,p = 0.001)。皮疹通常在新冠肺炎症状出现三天后(2-7 天)出现,大多为轻度至中度。常见的形态类型包括风团、红斑和丘疹/丘疱疹。50%的病例出现全身皮疹,而局部皮疹主要影响四肢。瘙痒是最常见的症状(>90%),尤其是出现风团的患者。临床管理包括局部类固醇乳膏和针对 COVID-19 的抗病毒治疗。大多数参与者(>95%)不需要后续行动。结论:与 Omicron 变异型 COVID-19 感染相关的皮疹主要影响年轻和中年女性,其特点是风团、红斑和丘疹/丘疹水疱疹。应将这些皮肤症状的早期识别和管理纳入 Omicron 变异 COVID-19 感染的整体治疗计划中。

问题与背景
旨在描述由 Omicron 变种引起的原发性 COVID-19 感染患者的皮肤表现及相应的治疗。
设计与对象
这项回顾性分析纳入了 94 名参与者,他们在感染 COVID-19 Omicron 变体后 1 个月内出现皮肤症状。
核心方法
常见的形态类型包括风团、红斑和丘疹/丘疱疹。
主要结果
50%的病例出现全身皮疹,而局部皮疹主要影响四肢。
解释与创新
大多数参与者(>95%)不需要后续行动。
科研与公卫意义
与 Omicron 变种 COVID-19 感染相关的皮疹主要影响年轻和中年女性,其特征是风团、红斑和丘疹/丘疹水疱疹。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Skin manifestations of primary COVID-19 infection with the omicron variant

Authors: Chuan Ma, Jiachen Sun, Zilian Liu, Chunlei Zhang, Yuansheng Chen, Jiawang Ma, Qing Ma
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Although cutaneous manifestations of COVID-19 have been increasingly documented across earlier variants, the dermatological characteristics specifically associated with the Omicron variant, particularly in the Chinese population, remain insufficiently characterized in the literature. OBJECTIVE: To characterize cutaneous manifestations in patients with primary COVID-19 infection caused by the Omicron variant and corresponding management. METHODS: 94 participants were included in this retrospective analysis, who developed dermatologic symptoms within one month of COVID-19 Omicron Variant infection. RESULTS: Mean age was 38 years old, ranging from 7 to 89; with 66% aged within 25-50. A significantly higher incidence of skin manifestations was reported among females (males vs. females, 1:2.76, p = 0.001). Skin rashes typically appeared three days after the onset of COVID symptoms (ranging between 2-7 days), which were mostly mild to moderate. Common morphological types included wheals, erythema, and papular/papulovesicular eruptions. Generalized rashes occurred in 50% of cases, while localized rashes mainly affected the extremities. Pruritus was the most frequent symptom (>90%), particularly in patients presenting with wheals. Clinical management included topical steroid creams and antiviral treatment for COVID-19. No follow-ups were required for most participants (>95%). CONCLUSION: Skin rashes associated with Omicron variant COVID-19 infection, predominantly affecting young and middle-aged women, are characterized by wheals, erythema, and papular/papulovesicular eruptions. Early identification and management of these cutaneous symptoms should be integrated in the overall treatment plan of Omicron variant COVID-19 infection.

一项多中心研究显示 PI3K 基因变异与 IL-6 水平升高和 COVID-19 严重后果相关

PI3K gene variants are associated with elevated IL-6 levels and COVID-19 severe outcomes in a multicenter study
作者： Bruna Ramos Tosta, João Victor Andrade Cruz, Ingrid Marins de Almeida, Laryssa Cardoso Calmon, Laiane da Cruz Pena, Hatilla Dos Santos Silva, Dyjaene de Oliveira Barbosa, Isa Rita Brito de Morais, Fabiane S. Reis-Goes, Nívia N. da Silva
摘要中文翻译由 SARS-CoV-2 引起的 COVID-19 导致临床结果差异很大。该病毒依赖宿主代谢途径,特别是 PI3K/AKT/mTOR 轴,调节细胞存活、代谢和病毒复制。该途径中的遗传变异可能会影响疾病的严重程度。本研究评估了 216 名轻度和 291 名重症 COVID-19 患者的 PI3K 基因变异,重点关注 rs3730089 (PIK3R1)、rs785468 (PIK3R3) 和 rs11121484 (PIK3CD)。评估外周血样本中的基因型,测量血浆样本中的炎性细胞因子(IL-6和TNF),并评估外周血单核细胞中的免疫细胞谱。rs11121484 的 C 等位基因与严重 COVID-19 的易感性相关,而 rs785468 和 rs3730089 与较高的死亡风险相关。多个风险等位基因的存在显示出对疾病结果的累积影响。rs3730089 GG 基因型患者的 IL-6 水平升高,风险等位基因携带者的非经典单核细胞和 CD4 + 记忆 T 细胞减少。这些发现表明 PI3K 单核苷酸变异 (SNV) 可能会加剧 COVID-19 中的炎症和免疫失调,从而导致严重的后果。总体而言,这些结果强调了 PI3K SNV 在驱动 COVID-19 患者过度炎症中的潜在病理作用,并可能有助于预测 COVID-19 的严重后果和更好的疾病预后。

多个风险等位基因的存在显示出对疾病结果的累积影响。

核心方法
评估外周血样本中的基因型,测量血浆样本中的炎性细胞因子(IL-6和TNF),并评估外周血单核细胞中的免疫细胞谱。
主要结果
rs11121484 的 C 等位基因与严重 COVID-19 的易感性相关,而 rs785468 和 rs3730089 与较高的死亡风险相关。
解释与创新

这些发现表明 PI3K 单核苷酸变异 (SNV) 可能会加剧 COVID-19 中的炎症和免疫失调,从而导致严重的后果。

PI3K gene variants are associated with elevated IL-6 levels and COVID-19 severe outcomes in a multicenter study

Authors: Bruna Ramos Tosta, João Victor Andrade Cruz, Ingrid Marins de Almeida, Laryssa Cardoso Calmon, Laiane da Cruz Pena, Hatilla Dos Santos Silva, Dyjaene de Oliveira Barbosa, Isa Rita Brito de Morais, Fabiane S. Reis-Goes, Nívia N. da Silva
Original Abstract
The COVID-19 caused by SARS-CoV-2 has resulted in widely variable clinical outcomes. The virus relies on host metabolic pathways particularly the PI3K/AKT/mTOR axis, which regulate cell survival, metabolism, and viral replication. Genetic variants whitin this pathway may influence disease severity. This study evaluated variants in PI3K genes among 216 mild and 291 severe COVID-19 patients, focusing on rs3730089 (PIK3R1), rs785468 (PIK3R3), and rs11121484 (PIK3CD). Genotypes were evaluted in peripheral blood samples, inflammatory cytokines (IL-6 and TNF) were measured in plasma samples and immune cell profiles were assessed in peripheral blood mononuclear cells. The C allele of rs11121484 was associated with susceptibility to severe COVID-19, while rs785468 and rs3730089 were correlated with higher mortality risk. The presence of multiple risk alleles showed a cumulative effect on disease outcome. Patients with the GG genotype of rs3730089 had increased IL-6 levels, and carriers of risk alleles displayed reduced non-classic monocytes and CD4 + memory T cells. These findings indicate that PI3K single-nucleotide variants (SNVs) may exacerbate inflammation and immune dysregulation in COVID-19, contributing to severe outcomes. Overall, these results highlight a potential pathological role for PI3K SNVs in driving hyperinflammation in COVID-19 patients and may contributes to predicting the severe outcome of COVID-19 and better disease prognosis.

Question and background
The presence of multiple risk alleles showed a cumulative effect on disease outcome.

This study evaluated variants in PI3K genes among 216 mild and 291 severe COVID-19 patients, focusing on rs3730089 (PIK3R1), rs785468 (PIK3R3), and rs11121484 (PIK3CD).
Core methods

Main results
The C allele of rs11121484 was associated with susceptibility to severe COVID-19, while rs785468 and rs3730089 were correlated with higher mortality risk.
Interpretation and novelty
Patients with the GG genotype of rs3730089 had increased IL-6 levels, and carriers of risk alleles displayed reduced non-classic monocytes and CD4 + memory T cells.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings indicate that PI3K single-nucleotide variants (SNVs) may exacerbate inflammation and immune dysregulation in COVID-19, contributing to severe outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

种族个体中 SARS-CoV-2 感染负担较高:来自加拿大血清学数据的证据

Higher SARS-CoV-2 infection burden among racialized individuals: evidence from Canadian serology data
作者： Simran Mann, Natalie Wilson, Clara Lee, David N. Fisman
摘要中文翻译简介:COVID-19 感染风险在不同种族群体中的分布并不均匀,其暴露程度受到社会和结构因素的影响。高传染性变种(即 Omicron)的出现极大地增加了感染率。然而,目前尚不清楚感染风险的种族差异在大流行期间是否消失或持续存在。目的:了解加拿大不同种族群体之间 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险有何差异,以及这些差异是否因 Omicron 变种而改变。方法:我们使用先前描述的动态易感感染模型分析了加拿大血液服务血清监测计划(2020 年 6 月至 2023 年 4 月)的横截面 SARS-CoV-2 血清阳性率数据,同时考虑了血清逆转。对前 Omicron 和 Omicron 时期(Omicron 的出现定义为 2021 年 12 月 26 日开始)的种族特定感染力进行了估计。结果:在 Omicron 之前,种族化个体的感染力高出 121%(IRR = 2.205;95% CI:2.115-2.299)。在 Omicron 时期,各种族群体内的感染率相对于前 Omicron 时期显着上升,白人群体中的感染率增加了 55.52 倍,种族群体中的感染率增加了 31.27 倍。尽管如此,在 Omicron 出现后,种族个体仍然受到不成比例的影响,其感染率比白人同行高 24%(IRR = 1.242;95% CI:1.231-1.253)。结论:Omicron 期间的广泛感染并没有导致流行病学公平,因为尽管粗略的血清流行率呈现趋同性,但种族化人群仍然面临更高的感染风险。

设计与对象
对前 Omicron 和 Omicron 时期(Omicron 的出现定义为 2021 年 12 月 26 日开始)的种族特定感染力进行了估计。
核心方法
我们使用先前描述的动态易感感染模型分析了加拿大血液服务血清监测计划(2020 年 6 月至 2023 年 4 月)的横截面 SARS-CoV-2 血清阳性率数据,同时考虑了血清逆转。
主要结果
高传染性变种(即 Omicron)的出现极大地增加了感染率。
解释与创新
在 Omicron 时期,各种族群体内的感染率相对于前 Omicron 时期显着上升,白人群体中的感染率增加了 55.52 倍,种族群体中的感染率增加了 31.27 倍。

Omicron 期间的广泛感染并没有导致流行病学公平,因为尽管粗略的血清流行率显示出趋同性,但种族人群仍然面临着更高的感染风险。

Higher SARS-CoV-2 infection burden among racialized individuals: evidence from Canadian serology data

Authors: Simran Mann, Natalie Wilson, Clara Lee, David N. Fisman
Original Abstract
INTRODUCTION: COVID-19 infection risk has not been evenly distributed across racial groups, with exposure being shaped by social and structural factors. The emergence of highly transmissible variants (i.e., Omicron) dramatically increased infection rates. However, it remains unclear whether racial disparities in infection risk disappeared or persisted over the course of the pandemic. OBJECTIVE: To understand how SARS-CoV-2 infection risk differed between racial groups in Canada and whether those disparities changed with the Omicron variant. METHODS: We analyzed cross-sectional SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence data from the Canadian Blood Services serosurveillance program (June 2020 to April 2023) using a previously described dynamic susceptible-infection model, while accounting for seroreversion. Race-specific force of infection was estimated for the pre-Omicron and Omicron periods (with the emergence of Omicron defined as beginning December 26, 2021). RESULTS: Prior to Omicron, racialized individuals had a 121% higher force of infection (IRR = 2.205; 95% CI: 2.115-2.299). During the Omicron period, infection rates rose significantly within each racial group relative to the pre-Omicron period, with a 55.52-fold increase among White individuals and a 31.27-fold increase among racialized individuals. Despite this, racialized individuals remained disproportionately affected following the emergence of Omicron, with 24% higher infection rates than those of their White counterparts (IRR = 1.242; 95% CI: 1.231-1.253). CONCLUSION: Widespread infection during Omicron did not result in epidemiologic equity, as racialized populations continued to experience higher infection risk despite crude seroprevalence depicting convergence.

Question and background
COVID-19 infection risk has not been evenly distributed across racial groups, with exposure being shaped by social and structural factors.
Design and population
Race-specific force of infection was estimated for the pre-Omicron and Omicron periods (with the emergence of Omicron defined as beginning December 26, 2021).
Core methods
We analyzed cross-sectional SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence data from the Canadian Blood Services serosurveillance program (June 2020 to April 2023) using a previously described dynamic susceptible-infection model, while accounting for seroreversion.
Main results
The emergence of highly transmissible variants (i.e., Omicron) dramatically increased infection rates.
Interpretation and novelty
During the Omicron period, infection rates rose significantly within each racial group relative to the pre-Omicron period, with a 55.52-fold increase among White individuals and a 31.27-fold increase among racialized individuals.
Scientific and public-health significance
Widespread infection during Omicron did not result in epidemiologic equity, as racialized populations continued to experience higher infection risk despite crude seroprevalence depicting convergence.
Limitations and evidence strength

使用 Track PCC 的大型被动监测队列将呼吸道感染作为一种后 COVID-19 疾病进行调查。

Investigating respiratory infection as a post-COVID-19 condition using a large passive surveillance cohort from Track PCC.
作者： Marina Oktapodas Feiler, Recai M Yucel, Bari J Dzomba, Resa M. Jones
摘要中文翻译背景:本研究使用来自跟踪儿童、青少年、成人不同人群中 COVID-19 后状况的负担、分布和影响 (Track PCC) 被动监测队列的数据,研究了 COVID 后呼吸道感染的危险因素。方法:这项回顾性研究纳入了宾夕法尼亚州费城 Temple Health 的 2020 年 3 月至 2022 年 12 月期间感染 SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) 的成年患者,并进行了 ≥ 90 天的随访。COVID-19 感染是通过实验室检测、账单代码或临床文件确定的。主要结果是通过帐单代码识别的新冠肺炎后呼吸道感染。预测因素包括社会、临床和新冠病毒相关的相关因素。使用 SuperMICE 对 17,539 个完整病例和多个估算数据集 (n = 45,513) 使用调整后的逻辑回归模型。结果:在完整病例分析中,未投保、Alpha 变异、总合并症和住院治疗与新冠肺炎后呼吸道疾病发生率较低相关(OR:0.02-0.99)。双重医疗保险/医疗补助和当前吸烟者的几率增加(OR:1.33-1.58)。估算分析显示,结果一致,并且还观察到,在接受医疗补助和疫苗接种次数较多的人群中,呼吸道感染的几率较高,而在任何非白人种族、西班牙裔人群中,呼吸道感染的几率较低。结论:Temple Track PCC 的研究结果确定了高风险人群,并强调了高级插补在基于监测的研究中的实用性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
SARS-CoV-2 感染的急性后遗症包括持续的呼吸道症状,但不同人群中新冠肺炎后呼吸道感染的危险因素仍未完全确定。这项研究利用大型被动监测队列,解决了新冠肺炎后呼吸道感染的社会和临床相关性的知识差距。
设计与对象
使用 Track PCC 被动监测系统对 2020 年 3 月至 2022 年 12 月期间宾夕法尼亚州费城 Temple Health 感染 SARS-CoV-2 的成年患者进行了一项回顾性队列研究,并进行了至少 90 天的随访。
核心方法
SARS-CoV-2 感染是通过实验室检测、帐单代码或临床文件确定的。主要结果是通过帐单代码识别的新冠肺炎后呼吸道感染。预测因素包括社会、临床和新冠病毒相关的相关因素。使用 SuperMICE 将调整后的逻辑回归模型应用于 17,539 个完整病例和多个估算数据集 (n = 45,513)。
主要结果
在完整病例分析中,未投保范围、Alpha 变异、总合并症和住院治疗与新冠肺炎后呼吸道疾病发生率较低相关(OR:0.02-0.99)。双重医疗保险/医疗补助和当前吸烟者的几率增加(OR:1.33-1.58)。估算分析证实了这些发现,还发现那些拥有医疗补助和疫苗接种次数较多的人的几率较高,而任何非白人种族或西班牙裔人的几率较低。
解释与创新
该研究确定了新冠肺炎后呼吸道感染的特定高危人群,并证明了先进的插补方法在增强基于监测的研究有效性方面的实用性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为新冠肺炎后呼吸道并发症高风险个体的针对性监测、风险分层和临床随访策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于被动监测数据和计费代码,这可能会引入错误分类和残留混杂,限制因果推断。由于回顾性设计和对行政数据的依赖,证据强度中等。

Investigating respiratory infection as a post-COVID-19 condition using a large passive surveillance cohort from Track PCC.

Authors: Marina Oktapodas Feiler, Recai M Yucel, Bari J Dzomba, Resa M. Jones
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: This study examined risk factors for post-COVID respiratory infection using data from the Tracking the Burden, Distribution, Impact of Tracking Post-COVID-19 Conditions in Diverse Populations for Children, Adolescents, Adults (Track PCC) passive surveillance cohort. METHODS: This retrospective study included adult Temple Health patients in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania with SARS-CoV-2 (COVID-19) infections from March 2020 to December 2022 and ≥ 90 days follow-up. COVID-19 infection was identified via laboratory testing, billing codes, or clinical documentation. The primary outcome was post-COVID respiratory infection identified by billing codes. Predictors included social, clinical, and COVID-related correlates. Adjusted logistic regression models were used on 17,539 complete cases and multiple imputed datasets (n = 45,513) with SuperMICE. RESULTS: In complete-case analysis, uninsured coverage, Alpha variant, total comorbidities, and hospitalization were associated with lower odds of respiratory post-COVID conditions (ORs: 0.02-0.99). Dual Medicare/Medicaid and current smokers, increased odds (ORs: 1.33-1.58). Imputed analyses showed consistent results with and additionally observed higher odds of respiratory infection among those with Medicaid and higher number of vaccinations, and lower odds among those of any non-white race, Hispanic ethnicity. CONCLUSIONS: Temple Track PCC findings identify high-risk populations and underscore the utility of advanced imputation in surveillance-based research.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Post-acute sequelae of SARS-CoV-2 infection include persistent respiratory symptoms, but risk factors for post-COVID respiratory infection remain incompletely characterized in diverse populations. This study addressed a knowledge gap on social and clinical correlates of post-COVID respiratory infection using a large passive surveillance cohort.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted using the Track PCC passive surveillance system among adult patients from Temple Health in Philadelphia, Pennsylvania, with SARS-CoV-2 infection from March 2020 to December 2022 and at least 90 days of follow-up.
Core methods
SARS-CoV-2 infection was identified via laboratory testing, billing codes, or clinical documentation. The primary outcome was post-COVID respiratory infection identified by billing codes. Predictors included social, clinical, and COVID-related correlates. Adjusted logistic regression models were applied to 17,539 complete cases and multiple imputed datasets (n = 45,513) using SuperMICE.
Main results
In complete-case analysis, uninsured coverage, Alpha variant, total comorbidities, and hospitalization were associated with lower odds of respiratory post-COVID conditions (ORs: 0.02–0.99). Dual Medicare/Medicaid and current smokers showed increased odds (ORs: 1.33–1.58). Imputed analyses confirmed these findings and additionally found higher odds among those with Medicaid and higher number of vaccinations, and lower odds among those of any non-white race or Hispanic ethnicity.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identifies specific high-risk populations for post-COVID respiratory infection and demonstrates the utility of advanced imputation methods in enhancing surveillance-based research validity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform targeted surveillance, risk stratification, and clinical follow-up strategies for individuals at higher risk of post-COVID respiratory complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on passive surveillance data and billing codes, which may introduce misclassification and residual confounding, limiting causal inference. Evidence strength is moderate due to retrospective design and reliance on administrative data.

COVID-19 疫苗接种与精神分裂症患者治疗依从性改善的关联:倾向评分匹配队列研究

Association of COVID-19 vaccination with improved treatment adherence in patients with schizophrenia: A propensity score-matched cohort study
作者： S. Jung, D. H. Lee, M. Hong, M.-K. Kim, W. Shin
摘要中文翻译精神分裂症患者是接种 COVID-19 疫苗的重点人群。然而,有关疫苗接种对这一弱势群体的现实临床影响的大规模数据有限。我们的目的是评估精神分裂症患者的 COVID-19 疫苗接种与治疗依从性、精神稳定性和 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险的关系。我们使用韩国国家健康保险服务和韩国疾病管理和预防机构数据库的关联数据进行了一项全国范围的回顾性倾向评分匹配队列研究(研究期限:2019年12月1日至2022年12月31日)。我们确定了 19-64 岁的精神分裂症患者,并将他们分为接种疫苗(三剂)和未接种疫苗的组。进行了 1:1 倾向评分匹配。主要结局是精神分裂症相关的住院治疗和急诊就诊。关键的次要结局包括药物依从性和诊断出的 SARS-CoV-2 感染。研究队列由 4629 对 (N = 9258) 组成。精神分裂症相关的住院或急诊就诊率没有观察到显着的组间差异。与未接种疫苗组相比,接种疫苗组的药物依从性下降幅度明显较小。与直觉相反,接种疫苗的组诊断出 SARS-CoV-2 感染的比率明显较高,这一发现最有可能归因于确定偏差,而不是疫苗功效降低。COVID-19 疫苗接种与精神不稳定无关,但与显着改善治疗依从性相关。我们的研究表明,疫苗接种可能在维持精神分裂症患者的临床连续性方面发挥支持作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
精神分裂症患者面临着较高的 COVID-19 风险,因此优先接种疫苗,但疫苗接种对该人群的实际临床影响仍没有充分记录。该研究通过评估 COVID-19 疫苗接种是否可以提高精神分裂症患者的治疗依从性、精神稳定性和 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险来解决这一知识差距。
设计与对象
韩国利用 2019 年 12 月 1 日至 2022 年 12 月 31 日关联的索赔和监测数据库进行了一项全国性回顾性倾向评分匹配队列研究。该队列包括 4629 对年龄 19-64 岁的精神分裂症患者,分为接种疫苗组(三剂)和未接种疫苗组。
核心方法
倾向评分匹配在已接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的患者之间建立了 1:1 的配对。主要结局是精神分裂症相关的住院治疗和急诊就诊,次要结局包括服药依从性和诊断出的 SARS-CoV-2 感染。
主要结果
在精神分裂症相关的住院或急诊就诊方面,各组之间没有观察到显着差异。与未接种疫苗组相比,接种疫苗组的药物依从性下降幅度明显较小。与直觉相反,接种疫苗组的 SARS-CoV-2 感染诊断率较高,这可能是由于确定偏差所致。
解释与创新
作者将疫苗接种解释为支持维持精神分裂症的临床连续性,并指出在不造成精神不稳定的情况下改善治疗依从性。新颖之处在于使用真实世界数据证明了弱势精神病人群的依从性益处。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果谨慎地表明,COVID-19 疫苗接种可能有助于维持精神分裂症患者的治疗连续性,为这一高危人群的疫苗接种政策和临床管理提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对行政数据的依赖限制了因果推断,并可能引入确定偏差,特别是在感染确定方面。由于这些限制和缺乏随机性,证据强度中等。

Association of COVID-19 vaccination with improved treatment adherence in patients with schizophrenia: A propensity score-matched cohort study

Authors: S. Jung, D. H. Lee, M. Hong, M.-K. Kim, W. Shin
Original Abstract
People with schizophrenia are a high-priority group for COVID-19 vaccination. However, large-scale data on the real-world clinical impact of vaccination in this vulnerable population is limited. We aimed to evaluate the association of COVID-19 vaccination with treatment adherence, psychiatric stability, and SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in patients with schizophrenia. We conducted a nationwide, retrospective, propensity score-matched cohort study (study period: December 1, 2019-December 31, 2022) using linked data from the National Health Insurance Service and Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency databases in South Korea. We identified patients with schizophrenia aged 19-64 years and stratified them into vaccinated (three doses) and unvaccinated groups. A 1:1 propensity score matching was performed. The primary outcomes were schizophrenia-related hospitalization and emergency department visits. Key secondary outcomes included medication adherence and diagnosed SARS-CoV-2 infection. The study cohort comprised 4629 pairs (N = 9258). No significant between-group differences were observed in the rates of schizophrenia-related hospitalization or emergency department visits. The vaccinated group showed a significantly smaller decline in medication adherence compared with the unvaccinated group. Counter-intuitively, the vaccinated group had a significantly higher rate of diagnosed SARS-CoV-2 infection, a finding most plausibly attributable to ascertainment bias rather than reduced vaccine efficacy. COVID-19 vaccination was not associated with psychiatric destabilization but was associated with significantly improved treatment adherence. Our study indicates that vaccination may play a supportive role in maintaining clinical continuity for individuals with schizophrenia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
People with schizophrenia face elevated risks from COVID-19 and are prioritized for vaccination, yet real-world clinical impacts of vaccination in this population remain insufficiently documented. The study addressed the knowledge gap by evaluating whether COVID-19 vaccination improves treatment adherence, psychiatric stability, and SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in patients with schizophrenia.
Design and population
A nationwide, retrospective, propensity score-matched cohort study was conducted in South Korea using linked claims and surveillance databases from December 1, 2019 to December 31, 2022. The cohort included 4629 pairs of patients with schizophrenia aged 19–64 years, stratified into vaccinated (three doses) and unvaccinated groups.
Core methods
Propensity score matching created 1:1 pairs between vaccinated and unvaccinated patients. Primary outcomes were schizophrenia-related hospitalization and emergency department visits, while secondary outcomes included medication adherence and diagnosed SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Main results
No significant differences were observed between groups in schizophrenia-related hospitalization or emergency department visits. The vaccinated group showed a significantly smaller decline in medication adherence than the unvaccinated group. Counter-intuitively, the vaccinated group had a higher rate of diagnosed SARS-CoV-2 infection, plausibly due to ascertainment bias.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret vaccination as supportive for maintaining clinical continuity in schizophrenia, noting improved treatment adherence without psychiatric destabilization. The novelty lies in demonstrating adherence benefits in a vulnerable psychiatric population using real-world data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings cautiously suggest that COVID-19 vaccination may help sustain treatment continuity in patients with schizophrenia, informing vaccination policies and clinical management in this high-risk group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on administrative data limit causal inference and may introduce ascertainment bias, particularly for infection ascertainment. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations and the absence of randomization.

COVID-19 后患者的健康相关生活质量概况和轨迹:潜在马尔可夫模型。

Health-related quality of life profiles and trajectories of patients with post COVID-19 condition: a latent Markov model.
作者： Brus IM, S. C. M. Heemskerk, S. Polinder, D. Nieboer, P. Tieleman, S. Biere-Rafi, Haagsma JA
摘要中文翻译背景 本研究的目的是根据 PCC 患者的健康相关生活质量 (HRQoL) 概况(即受影响维度和每个维度问题严重程度的差异)来识别具有相似健康状况的亚组,检查这些亚组之间随时间的转变,并检查与社会人口学和医学特征的关联。方法 在这项纵向队列研究中,使用潜在马尔可夫模型对通过两次在线调查收集的 5,737 名 PCC 患者的数据进行了分析。每个 EQ-5D-5L 维度的二分 HRQoL 分数用作确定潜在状态的指标,并将协变量添加到模型中。结果选择了具有六个潜在状态的模型。最大的州(由 31% 的受访者组成)的特点是在所有维度上出现问题的概率都很高,而最小的州(7%)的特点是除了日常活动之外,在所有维度上出现问题的概率都很低。其余四种状态的特征都是在日常活动和疼痛/不适方面出现问题的可能性很高,但在活动能力、自我护理和焦虑/抑郁方面出现问题的概率有所不同。转换到不同状态的概率很低,受影响维度较少的状态最稳定。女性、年轻受访者、教育水平较低的受访者以及患有合并症的受访者更有可能处于受影响程度更大的州。结论 不同 HRQoL 概况的识别表明,受影响的 HRQoL 维度以及两个测量点之间观察到的变化存在显着的异质性。

Health-related quality of life profiles and trajectories of patients with post COVID-19 condition: a latent Markov model.

Authors: Brus IM, S. C. M. Heemskerk, S. Polinder, D. Nieboer, P. Tieleman, S. Biere-Rafi, Haagsma JA
Original Abstract
Background The aim of this study was to identify subgroups with similar health states based on health-related quality of life (HRQoL) profile (i.e. differences in affected dimensions and severity of problems per dimension) among PCC patients, to examine transitions between these subgroups over time, and to examine the association with sociodemographic and medical characteristics. Methods In this longitudinal cohort study, data from 5,737 PCC patients, collected through two online surveys, were analysed using a latent Markov model. Dichotomized HRQoL scores per EQ-5D-5L dimension were used as indicators to determine latent states and covariates were added into the model. Results A model with six latent states was selected. The largest state, consisting of 31% of respondents, was characterized by a high probability of problems on all dimensions, whereas the smallest state (7%) was characterized by a low probability of problems on all dimensions, except usual activities. The remaining four states were all characterized by a high probability of problems on usual activities and pain/discomfort, but differed based on the probability of problems on the dimensions mobility, self-care and anxiety/depression. The probability of transitioning to a different state was low, with states with fewer affected dimensions being most stable. Women, younger respondents, those with a lower educational level, and those with comorbidity were more likely to be in states with more affected dimensions. Conclusions The identification of different HRQoL profiles shows that there is substantial heterogeneity in the HRQoL dimensions that are affected and in observed changes between two measurement points.

CCR2炎症通路是改善实验性COVID-19严重疾病和肺部炎症的目标

The CCR2 inflammatory pathway is a target for improving severe disease and pulmonary inflammation in experimental COVID-19
作者： Yvette Kazungu, Shrilakshmi Hegde, Parul Sharma, Amy E. Marriott, Andrew Steven, Jesús Reiné, Jessica L Dagley, Matthias Mack, James P. Stewart, Joseph D. Turner
摘要中文翻译摘要 SARS-CoV2 可诱发急性呼吸窘迫综合征 (ARDS),这是由过度炎症性肺部免疫反应失调引起的。在这里,我们使用 SARS-CoV2 的角质形成细胞 18 人源化血管紧张素转换酶 2 (K18-hACE2) 小鼠模型,其中 CoV2 刺突蛋白受体 hACE-2 的表达仅限于上皮细胞,以表征 Delta 分离株 SARS-CoV2 (Δ B.1.617.2) 鼻内感染后的炎症性肺部免疫反应。通过聚焦转录物分析、炎症蛋白阵列和多色流式细胞术进行的免疫分析证实,感染后第 5 天,小鼠肺部的 COVID-19 临床相关标记物(IL-6、GM-CSF、中性粒细胞、炎症单核细胞)显着升高,并且瑞德西韦抗病毒活性代谢物 (GS441524) 治疗显着改变了 SARS-CoV2Δ 病毒载量和肺部特异性炎症。在针对 SARS-CoV2Δ 的集中人类感染反应阵列中,CCR2 趋化因子配体 (CCL2/7/8) 属于上调前 5% 的肺部转录本之一。在 SARS-CoV2Δ 感染的肺部的蛋白质检测中,CCL2 被证实升高。为了解决炎症细胞募集到肺部介导疾病的 CCR2 途径的功能相关性,每天在感染点给小鼠施用抗 CCR2 抗体,持续长达六天。抗CCR2治疗的小鼠表现出显着改善的福利评分,免受体重减轻和改良性骨髓性肺炎的影响,并且显示出肺部细胞因子和趋化因子反应显着减弱,尽管不影响肺部病毒载量。我们的数据支持修改 CCR2 依赖性细胞募集在治疗病毒诱导的 ARDS 中的治疗益处。

The CCR2 inflammatory pathway is a target for improving severe disease and pulmonary inflammation in experimental COVID-19

Authors: Yvette Kazungu, Shrilakshmi Hegde, Parul Sharma, Amy E. Marriott, Andrew Steven, Jesús Reiné, Jessica L Dagley, Matthias Mack, James P. Stewart, Joseph D. Turner
Original Abstract
Abstract SARS-CoV2 can induce an acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS), provoked by a dysregulated hyper-inflammatory pulmonary immune response. Here we used the keratinocyte-18 humanized angiotensin converting enzyme-2 (K18-hACE2) mouse model of SARS-CoV2, where expression of the CoV2 spike protein receptor, hACE-2, is restricted to epithelia, to characterize inflammatory pulmonary immune responses post-intranasal infections with the delta isolate SARS-CoV2(Δ B.1.617.2). Immune-profiling by focused transcript analysis, inflammatory protein array, and multi-color flow cytometry, confirmed that clinically relevant markers of COVID-19 (IL-6, GM-CSF, neutrophils, inflammatory monocytes) were significantly elevated in lungs of mice at day 5 post-infection and that remdesivir anti-viral active metabolite (GS441524) treatment significantly modified SARS-CoV2Δ viral loads and pulmonary-specific inflammation. Chemokine ligands of CCR2 (CCL2/7/8) were amongst the top 5% upregulated pulmonary transcripts in a focused human infection response array to SARS-CoV2Δ. CCL2 was confirmed as elevated in protein assays in SARS-CoV2Δ infected lungs. To address functional relevance of the CCR2 pathway of inflammatory cell recruitment to the lungs mediating disease, mice were administered with anti-CCR2 antibody daily at point of infection for up to six days. Anti-CCR2 treated mice showed significant improved welfare scores, were protected from weight loss, modified myeloid pneumonitis, and displayed significantly blunted cytokine and chemokine response in the lungs, despite not affecting pulmonary viral loads. Our data supports therapeutic benefit of modifying CCR2-dependent cell recruitment in the treatment of viral-induced ARDS.

疱疹病毒再激活与重症 ICU 感染 COVID-19 患者的死亡率相关:来自 455 例病例的回顾性单中心分析的见解

Herpesvirus reactivation is associated with mortality in critically ill ICU patients with COVID-19: Insights from a retrospective single-center analysis of 455 cases
作者： Stefanie Michel, Cilian Kempf, Nina Pirschtat, Frank Herbstreit, Bettina Budeus, Sebastian Voigt, Jutta Dedy, Thorsten Brenner, Simon Dubler
摘要中文翻译背景:患有 COVID-19 的危重患者经常出现病毒再激活,但 1 型单纯疱疹病毒 (HSV-1) 和巨细胞病毒 (CMV) 的临床意义仍然存在争议。方法:我们对 2020 年 3 月至 2021 年 12 月期间连续 455 名确诊 SARS-CoV-2 感染的重症监护病房 (ICU) 患者进行了一项回顾性单中心研究。患者在全血和呼吸道样本中接受了 HSV-1 和 CMV 筛查。使用多变量模型独立调查与死亡率相关的危险因素。结果:平均年龄为 57 岁(范围:13-94 岁),其中 316 名(69%)为男性。在 ICU 住院时间中位数 14 (0-90) 天期间,164 名 (36%) 患者发生病毒再激活(HSV-1 或 CMV 或两者)。在 57 名 (13%) 患者中检测到 HSV-1 和 CMV 共同再激活,在 54 名 (12%) 患者中观察到单独 HSV-1 再激活,在 53 名 (12%) 患者中观察到单独 CMV 再激活。从入住 ICU 到病毒检测的中位时间为 4 天,并且随着 ICU 停留时间的延长而逐步增加。HSV-1 检测与 ICU 生存率下降相关(32% 对比 67%;p < 0.001),多变量调整后这一情况仍然显着。HSV-1(而非 CMV)的检测与有创机械通气、静脉-静脉体外膜氧合治疗以及肾脏替代治疗的需求增加相关。较高的中性粒细胞-淋巴细胞比率与 ICU 死亡风险增加独立相关,并与 HSV-1 和 CMV 检测相关。结论:疱疹病毒再激活在 COVID-19 危重患者中很常见。HSV-1 重新激活与更严重的病程和 ICU 死亡率增加相关。

Herpesvirus reactivation is associated with mortality in critically ill ICU patients with COVID-19: Insights from a retrospective single-center analysis of 455 cases

Authors: Stefanie Michel, Cilian Kempf, Nina Pirschtat, Frank Herbstreit, Bettina Budeus, Sebastian Voigt, Jutta Dedy, Thorsten Brenner, Simon Dubler
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Critically ill patients with COVID-19 frequently develop viral reactivation, yet the clinical significance of herpes simplex virus type 1 (HSV-1) and cytomegalovirus (CMV) remains a matter of debate. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective single-center study of 455 consecutive intensive care unit (ICU) patients with confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection between March 2020 and December 2021. Patients underwent screening for HSV-1 and CMV in whole blood and respiratory samples. A multivariable model was used to independently investigate risk factors associated with mortality. RESULTS: The mean age was 57 years (range: 13-94 years), and 316 (69%) were male. During a median ICU stay of 14 (0-90) days, viral reactivation (either HSV-1 or CMV or both) occurred in 164 (36%) patients. HSV-1 and CMV co-reactivation was detected in 57 (13%) patients, isolated HSV-1 reactivation was observed in 54 (12%) patients and isolated CMV reactivation in 53 (12%) patients. Median time from ICU admission to viral detection was 4 days, with a stepwise increase with longer ICU stay. Detection of HSV-1 was associated with decreased ICU survival (32% versus 67%; p < 0.001) which remained significant after multivariable adjustment. Detection of HSV-1 but not CMV was associated with an increased need for invasive mechanical ventilation, veno-venous extracorporeal membrane oxygenation therapy, and the need for renal replacement therapy. Higher neutrophil-lymphocyte ratios were independently associated with an increased risk of ICU mortality and correlated with detection of HSV-1 and CMV. CONCLUSION: Herpesvirus reactivation was common in critically ill patients with COVID-19. HSV-1 reactivation was associated with a more severe course of disease and increased ICU mortality.

Question and background
Critically ill patients with COVID-19 frequently develop viral reactivation, yet the clinical significance of herpes simplex virus type 1 (HSV-1) and cytomegalovirus (CMV) remains a matter of debate.
Design and population
Patients underwent screening for HSV-1 and CMV in whole blood and respiratory samples.
Core methods
A multivariable model was used to independently investigate risk factors associated with mortality.
Main results
Detection of HSV-1 was associated with decreased ICU survival (32% versus 67%; p < 0.001) which remained significant after multivariable adjustment.
Interpretation and novelty
Higher neutrophil-lymphocyte ratios were independently associated with an increased risk of ICU mortality and correlated with detection of HSV-1 and CMV.
Scientific and public-health significance
Herpesvirus reactivation was common in critically ill patients with COVID-19.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

机构质量和大流行抵御能力:关于 COVID-19 期间健康和经济结果的跨国证据。

Institutional quality and pandemic resilience: cross-country evidence on health and economic outcomes during COVID-19.
作者： Diaz DA, Cristián Larroulet, Andres Marticorena, Diego A. Díaz
摘要中文翻译背景 COVID-19 大流行暴露了死亡率、疫苗接种推广和经济复苏方面的巨大跨国差异。我们研究了更强大的大流行前机构是否与更好的大流行表现相关,以及疫苗接种的推出是否有助于解释这种关系的部分原因。我们将 2019 年世界银行全球治理指标与 COVID-19 结果、政策措施、世界发展指标和 V-Dem 选举民主数据相结合,估计跨国回归和使用截至 2021 年 9 月疫苗接种的临时有序探索性调解模型以及 2021 年第四季度累计的死亡人数。结果在完整的多变量规范中,WGI 综合数据每增加一个单位,报告的死亡人数就会减少 384 人。到 2021 年底,每百万人的 COVID-19 死亡人数、每百万人的超额死亡人数减少 1,860 人,全面疫苗接种覆盖率提高 15.5%。使用 V-Dem 选举民主控制、WGI 主成分测量、特定时间结果和影响力诊断,对这些关联进行稳健性检查。制度质量与 2020 年 GDP 增长并不存在显着相关性;它与 2021 年 GDP 增长的正相关仍然存在,但在控制 OxCGRT 经济支持政策后,其正相关减弱。探索性中介分解与疫苗接种的间接路径一致,约占机构质量与 2021 年末死亡率之间总关联的 35%。结论 大流行前的机构质量始终与大流行健康结果和疫苗部署相关,而经济关联则更具阶段性。这些发现是观察性的,不应被解读为明确的因果估计,但它们强调了治理能力与未来大流行防范的相关性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 COVID-19 大流行暴露了死亡率、疫苗接种推广和经济复苏方面的巨大跨国差异。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
我们将 2019 年世界银行全球治理指标与 COVID-19 结果、政策措施、世界发展指标和 V-Dem 选举民主数据相结合,利用 2021 年 9 月之前的疫苗接种情况和 2021 年第四季度的死亡人数来估计跨国回归和按时间排序的探索性调解模型。
主要结果
结果 在完整的多变量规范中,到 2021 年底,WGI 复合数据每增加一个单位,每百万人报告的 COVID-19 死亡人数将减少 384 人,每百万人超额死亡人数将减少 1,860 人,并且全面疫苗接种覆盖率将提高 15.5%。
解释与创新
结论 大流行前的机构质量始终与大流行健康结果和疫苗部署相关,而经济关联则更具阶段性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现是观察性的,不应被解读为明确的因果估计,但它们强调了治理能力与未来大流行防范的相关性。
局限与证据强度
制度质量与 2020 年 GDP 增长并不存在显着相关性;它与 2021 年 GDP 增长的正相关仍然存在,但在控制 OxCGRT 经济支持政策后,其正相关减弱。

Institutional quality and pandemic resilience: cross-country evidence on health and economic outcomes during COVID-19.

Authors: Diaz DA, Cristián Larroulet, Andres Marticorena, Diego A. Díaz
Original Abstract
Background The COVID-19 pandemic exposed large cross-country differences in mortality, vaccination rollout, and economic recovery. We examine whether stronger pre-pandemic institutions were associated with better pandemic performance and whether vaccination rollout helps explain part of that relationship. We combine 2019 World Bank Worldwide Governance Indicators with COVID-19 outcomes, policy measures, World Development Indicators, and V-Dem electoral democracy data, estimating cross-country regressions and a temporally ordered exploratory mediation model using vaccination by September 2021 and deaths accrued in the fourth quarter of 2021. Results In full multivariable specifications, a one-unit increase in the WGI composite is associated with 384 fewer reported COVID-19 deaths per million, 1,860 fewer excess deaths per million, and 15.5% points higher full vaccination coverage by end-2021. These associations remain in robustness checks using V-Dem electoral democracy controls, WGI principal-component measures, timing-specific outcomes, and influence diagnostics. Institutional quality is not robustly associated with GDP growth in 2020; its positive association with GDP growth in 2021 persists but is weaker after controlling for OxCGRT economic-support policies. The exploratory mediation decomposition is consistent with an indirect path through vaccination representing about 35% of the total association between institutional quality and late-2021 mortality. Conclusions Pre-pandemic institutional quality was consistently associated with pandemic health outcomes and vaccine deployment, while economic associations were more phase-specific. The findings are observational and should not be read as definitive causal estimates, but they underscore the relevance of governance capacity for future pandemic preparedness.

土耳其 COVID-19 期后住院儿童严重百日咳的临床特征和预测因素:一项多中心研究。

Clinical characteristics and predictors of severe pertussis in hospitalized children after the COVID-19 period in Türkiye: a multicenter study.
作者： Sevliya Ocal-Demir, Pınar Canizci Erdemli, Cakmak Taskin E, Tekin Yilmaz A, Semra Bayturan Şen, Deniz Ergün, Hincal Ozbakir, Berna Çetin Kahraman, Canan Özlü, Berker Okay
摘要中文翻译在放松 COVID-19 措施后,百日咳在全球范围内卷土重来,凸显了其作为公共卫生问题的持续相关性,但与儿童严重疾病相关的因素的数据仍然有限。本研究确定了严重百日咳的危险因素,以改善早期识别和临床管理。这项多中心回顾性研究分析了土耳其 47 个中心(2023-2024 年)因实验室确诊的百日咳住院的 839 名儿童的人口统计学、临床和实验室数据。严重疾病被定义为需要呼吸支持或发生脑病和/或心血管功能障碍。单变量比较后,使用中心调整的多变量逻辑回归在疫苗合格组(> 2个月)和不合格组(≤ 2个月)中评估显着变量。患者的中位年龄为2个月(IQR 1.5-5.0),77.8%小于6个月。26.1%的病例出现严重疾病。在单变量分析中,年龄较小、拒绝疫苗、难民身份、喂养困难、呼吸暂停、呼吸窘迫、白细胞增多、病毒合并感染和入院时胸部 X 光检查结果异常与严重疾病相关。在 ≤ 2 个月的婴儿中,呼吸暂停(OR = 3.74;p 3 /μL 增加;p = 0.003)与严重疾病独立相关。在大于 2 个月的儿童中,呼吸窘迫(OR = 13.59;p < 0.001)和未及时接种疫苗(OR = 2.60;p = 0.019)是严重疾病的独立预测因素。总体而言,21.5% 的患者需要重症监护,24.3% 需要通气,死亡率为 2.4%。结论:重症百日咳主要影响小婴儿,呼吸暂停、呼吸窘迫和白细胞增多被认为是重症百日咳的主要预测因素。此外,不完整的疫苗接种与疾病严重程度的增加有关。

Clinical characteristics and predictors of severe pertussis in hospitalized children after the COVID-19 period in Türkiye: a multicenter study.

Authors: Sevliya Ocal-Demir, Pınar Canizci Erdemli, Cakmak Taskin E, Tekin Yilmaz A, Semra Bayturan Şen, Deniz Ergün, Hincal Ozbakir, Berna Çetin Kahraman, Canan Özlü, Berker Okay
Original Abstract
Pertussis resurged globally, after the relaxation of COVID-19 measures, underscoring its continued relevance as a public health concern, yet data remain limited on the factors associated with severe disease in children. The present study identified risk factors for severe pertussis to improve early recognition and clinical management. This multicenter retrospective study analyzed the demographic, clinical, and laboratory data of the 839 children hospitalized with laboratory-confirmed pertussis across 47 centers in Türkiye (2023-2024). Severe disease was defined as the need for respiratory support or the development of encephalopathy and/or cardiovascular dysfunction. After univariate comparison, significant variables were evaluated using center-adjusted multivariable logistic regression in vaccine-eligible (> 2 months) and non-eligible (≤ 2 months) groups. The patient's median age was 2 months (IQR 1.5-5.0), and 77.8% were younger than 6 months. Severe disease occurred in 26.1% of cases. In univariate analysis, younger age, vaccine refusal, refugee status, feeding difficulties, apnea, respiratory distress, leukocytosis, viral co-infection, and abnormal chest X-ray findings at admission were associated with severe disease. In infants ≤ 2 months, apnea (OR = 3.74; p 3 /μL increase; p = 0.003) were independently associated with severe disease. In children > 2 months, respiratory distress (OR = 13.59; p < 0.001) and not being up-to-date with vaccination (OR = 2.60; p = 0.019) were independent predictors of severe disease. Overall, 21.5% of patients required intensive care, 24.3% needed ventilation, and mortality was 2.4%. Conclusion: Severe pertussis predominantly affects young infants, and apnea, respiratory distress, and leukocytosis were identified as major predictors of severe disease. Furthermore, incomplete vaccination is associated with increased disease severity.

Core methods

Main results
In univariate analysis, younger age, vaccine refusal, refugee status, feeding difficulties, apnea, respiratory distress, leukocytosis, viral co-infection, and abnormal chest X-ray findings at admission were associated with severe disease.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

COVID-19 和时间知觉:大流行后世界中长期的短期时间知觉

COVID-19 and temporal perception: prolonged short-term time perception in a post-pandemic world
作者： Wenjie Liu, Tian Yuan, Li Wang, Yi Jiang
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 大流行深刻地改变了日常生活,极大地影响了心理健康。据报道,这种负面事件会改变对时间的主观体验,例如日期的混合,反映了相对粗糙尺度上长期时间感知的扭曲。然而,COVID-19 大流行对细尺度短期时间感知的影响却很少受到关注。在这里,我们深入研究了这个尚未充分探索的领域,证明大流行作为一种高度唤醒的负面事件,延长了我们对短时间间隔的感知。使用两个会话持续时间歧视范式,我们发现与 COVID-19 相关的图像被感知为明显长于相同持续时间的中性图片。与情绪引起的时间扭曲不同,这种扩张在整个过程中是一致的,与高度的唤醒相关,不受焦虑的影响,并且表现出持久的影响——甚至在最初爆发四年后仍然存在。总而言之,我们的研究结果强调了 COVID-19 刺激引起的时间扩张,扩展了我们对高唤醒负面事件如何与认知过程(特别是时间感知)相互作用的理解。

问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行深刻地改变了日常生活,极大地影响了心理健康。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
使用两个会话持续时间歧视范式,我们发现与 COVID-19 相关的图像被感知为明显长于相同持续时间的中性图片。
主要结果
据报道,这种负面事件会改变对时间的主观体验,例如日期的混合,反映了相对粗糙尺度上长期时间感知的扭曲。
解释与创新
与情绪引起的时间扭曲不同,这种扩张在整个过程中是一致的,与高度的唤醒相关,不受焦虑的影响,并且表现出持久的影响——甚至在最初爆发四年后仍然存在。
科研与公卫意义
总而言之,我们的研究结果强调了 COVID-19 刺激引起的时间扩张,扩展了我们对高唤醒负面事件如何与认知过程(特别是时间感知)相互作用的理解。

COVID-19 and temporal perception: prolonged short-term time perception in a post-pandemic world

Authors: Wenjie Liu, Tian Yuan, Li Wang, Yi Jiang
Original Abstract
The COVID-19 pandemic has profoundly restructured daily life, significantly impacting mental health. This negative event has been reported to alter subjective experience of time, such as the blending of days, reflecting distortions in long-term time perception over relatively coarse scales. However, the influence of the COVID-19 pandemic on short-term time perception over fine scales has received much less attention. Here we delve into this underexplored territory, demonstrating that the pandemic, as a high-arousal negative event, lengthens our perception of short time intervals. Using a two-session duration discrimination paradigm, we showed that COVID-19 related images were perceived as significantly longer than neutral pictures of the same duration. Unlike emotion-induced temporal distortions, this dilation was consistent across sessions, correlated with heightened arousal, remained unaffected by anxiety, and exhibited a long-lasting effect - persisting even four years after the initial outbreak. Taken together, our findings highlight a temporal dilation induced by COVID-19 stimuli, extending our understanding of how high-arousal negative events interact with cognitive processes, particularly time perception.

COVID-19 后呼吸道病毒的死灰复燃:来自克什米尔儿科 SARI 的分子见解。

The Resurgence of Respiratory Viruses After COVID-19: Molecular Insights from Pediatric SARI in Kashmir.
作者： Sanam Rasool Wani, Bashir Ahmad Fomda, Mushtaq Ahmad Bhat, Gulnaz Bashir, Shuguftah Roohi, Suraiya Mir, Fozia Subzar, Sheikh Imtiyaz
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道病毒是儿童严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)的主要原因。印度北部的分子流行病学数据仍然有限,特别是在后 COVID-19 时期。本研究旨在确定克什米尔住院儿童 SARI 患者中呼吸道病毒病原体的患病率和分布。方法:2023 年 10 月至 2025 年 4 月在斯利那加谢尔克什米尔医学科学研究所进行了一项前瞻性研究。纳入符合世界卫生组织SARI病例定义的年龄≤15岁的儿童。收集鼻咽和/或口咽样本,并使用实时聚合酶链反应检测 SARS-CoV-2、甲型和乙型流感、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) A 和 B、腺病毒、人类偏肺病毒、鼻病毒和副流感病毒。结果:共纳入 326 例儿童 SARI 病例。200 名患者(61.3%)中至少检测到一种呼吸道病毒。RSV 是最常检测到的病毒,其中 RSV-B 在 111 例(34.0%)中检出,RSV-A 在 26 例(7.9%)中检出。流感病毒所占比例较小,其中甲型H1N1流感12例(3.6%),甲型H3N2流感6例(1.8%),乙型流感(维多利亚系)21例(6.4%)。检测到的其他病毒包括腺病毒、副流感病毒、鼻病毒和人类偏肺病毒。大多数感染发生在冬季,主要影响婴儿。结论:克什米尔儿童 SARI 病例中呼吸道病毒占很大比例,RSV 成为大流行后时期的主要病原体。加强分子监测可以改善诊断管理并指导有针对性的预防策略。

The Resurgence of Respiratory Viruses After COVID-19: Molecular Insights from Pediatric SARI in Kashmir.

Authors: Sanam Rasool Wani, Bashir Ahmad Fomda, Mushtaq Ahmad Bhat, Gulnaz Bashir, Shuguftah Roohi, Suraiya Mir, Fozia Subzar, Sheikh Imtiyaz
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Respiratory viruses are major contributors to severe acute respiratory infections (SARI) in children. Data on their molecular epidemiology in northern India remains limited, particularly in the post-COVID-19 period. This study aimed to determine the prevalence and distribution of respiratory viral pathogens among hospitalized pediatric SARI patients in Kashmir. METHODS: A prospective study was conducted from October 2023 to April 2025 at Sher-i-Kashmir Institute of Medical Sciences, Srinagar. Children aged ≤15 years meeting the World Health Organization SARI case definition were enrolled. Nasopharyngeal and/or oropharyngeal samples were collected and tested using real-time polymerase chain reaction assays for SARS-CoV-2, Influenza A and B, Respiratory Syncytial virus (RSV) A and B, Adenovirus, Human metapneumovirus, Rhinovirus, and Para-influenza viruses. RESULTS: A total of 326 pediatric SARI cases were included. At least one respiratory virus was detected in 200 patients (61.3%). RSV was the most frequently detected virus, with RSV-B identified in 111 cases (34.0%) and RSV-A in 26 cases (7.9%). Influenza viruses accounted for a smaller proportion, including influenza A(H1N1) in 12 cases (3.6%), influenza A(H3N2) in 6 cases (1.8%), and influenza B (Victoria lineage) in 21 cases (6.4%). Other viruses detected included Adenovirus, Para-influenza virus, Rhinovirus, and Human metapneumovirus. Most infections occurred during winter months and predominantly affected infants. CONCLUSION: Respiratory viruses account for a substantial proportion of pediatric SARI cases in Kashmir, with RSV emerging as the predominant pathogen in the post-pandemic period. Strengthening molecular surveillance may improve diagnostic stewardship and guide targeted prevention strategies.
