Main results
At least one respiratory virus was detected in 200 patients (61.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
Most infections occurred during winter months and predominantly affected infants.

Limitations and evidence strength

在多次暴露队列中接种 XBB.1.5 疫苗后针对 SARS-CoV-2 变体的中和抗体的持久性和广度

Durability and Breadth of Neutralizing Antibodies Against SARS-CoV-2 Variants Following XBB.1.5 Vaccination in a Multiply Exposed Cohort
作者： Wei Wang, Emilie Goguet, Sabrina Lusvarghi, Stephanie Paz, Leeza Shrestha, Russell Vassell, Simon Pollett, Edward Mitre, Carol D. Weiss
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 对疫苗接种和感染的广泛免疫力降低了 COVID-19 的发病率和死亡率,但这种免疫力因人群而异。了解重复的抗原暴露如何影响抗体反应有助于为未来的疫苗接种策略提供信息。方法 我们对 XBB.1.5 疫苗接种后 1 个月和 6 个月收集的血清样本中的中和抗体反应进行了表征,这些样本来自 25 名具有不同且复杂的疫苗接种史和报告感染史的医护人员。使用慢病毒假病毒评估了一系列变体的中和活性,从前 Omicron 到最近的 Omicron JN.1 亚系,以及不同的 BA.3.2 变体。根据参与者记录的疫苗接种情况和报告的感染史,将其分为五个暴露组。结果 XBB.1.5 疫苗接种引发了广泛的中和反应,相对于疫苗匹配的 XBB.1.5 和更新的变体,对先前遇到的抗原具有强烈的增强作用。尽管较小的亚组规模和相当大的组内异质性排除了关于既往暴露史的影响的明确结论,但几何平均中和滴度在不同暴露组之间通常具有可比性。六个月时,所有变体的滴度下降了 36-62%。针对 Omicron 前变体的滴度仍然最高,针对 JN.1 亚系变体的滴度最低,其中一些下降到非常低的水平。结论 在这个先前广泛接触过抗原的队列中,在 XBB.1.5 疫苗接种后观察到了广泛的中和抗体谱,尽管这些反应在六个月后大幅减弱。观察到的针对新兴变种的交叉中和抗体的减弱凸显了维持持久保护的挑战,并支持持续监测抗体反应的必要性,以指导基于证据的 COVID-19 疫苗更新。

提供的证据没有明确说明研究问题或其基本原理。

核心方法
使用慢病毒假病毒评估了一系列变体的中和活性,从前 Omicron 到最近的 Omicron JN.1 亚系,以及不同的 BA.3.2 变体。
主要结果
结果 XBB.1.5 疫苗接种引发了广泛的中和反应,相对于疫苗匹配的 XBB.1.5 和更新的变体,对先前遇到的抗原具有强烈的增强作用。
解释与创新
观察到的针对新兴变种的交叉中和抗体的减弱凸显了维持持久保护的挑战,并支持持续监测抗体反应的必要性,以指导基于证据的 COVID-19 疫苗更新。
科研与公卫意义

局限与证据强度
尽管较小的亚组规模和相当大的组内异质性排除了关于既往暴露史的影响的明确结论,但几何平均中和滴度在不同暴露组之间通常具有可比性。

Durability and Breadth of Neutralizing Antibodies Against SARS-CoV-2 Variants Following XBB.1.5 Vaccination in a Multiply Exposed Cohort

Authors: Wei Wang, Emilie Goguet, Sabrina Lusvarghi, Stephanie Paz, Leeza Shrestha, Russell Vassell, Simon Pollett, Edward Mitre, Carol D. Weiss
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Widespread immunity from vaccination and infection has reduced COVID-19 morbidity and mortality, but this immunity varies across the population. Understanding how repeated antigenic exposures influence antibody responses helps to inform future vaccination strategies. Methods We characterized neutralizing antibody responses in serum samples collected one and six months after XBB.1.5 vaccination from 25 healthcare workers with varying, complex histories of vaccination and reported infections. Neutralizing activity was assessed against a range of variants, from pre-Omicron to recent Omicron JN.1 sublineage, and divergent BA.3.2 variants using lentiviral pseudoviruses. Participants were stratified into five exposure groups based on their documented vaccination and reported infection history. Results XBB.1.5 vaccination elicited broad neutralizing responses, with strong boosting against previously encountered antigens relative to vaccine-matched XBB.1.5 and newer variants. Geometric mean neutralization titers were generally comparable across exposure groups, though small subgroup sizes and considerable intra-group heterogeneity precluded definitive conclusions about the influence of prior exposure history. At six months, titers declined by 36-62% across all variants. Titers remained highest against pre-Omicron variants and were lowest against JN.1 sublineage variants, with some falling to very low levels. Conclusions In this cohort with extensive prior antigenic exposures, broad neutralizing antibody profiles were observed following XBB.1.5 vaccination, though these responses waned substantially after six months. The observed waning of cross-neutralizing antibodies against emerging variants underscores the challenge of maintaining durable protection and supports the need for continued monitoring of antibody responses to guide evidence-based updates to COVID-19 vaccines.

识别调节肺细胞增强子报告活性的严重 COVID-19 风险变异

Identifying severe COVID-19 risk variants modulating enhancer reporter activity in lung cells
作者： Giovanna Weykopf, Wendy A. Bickmore, Simon C. Biddie, Elias T. Friman
摘要中文翻译常见的遗传变异会增加复杂人类疾病的风险。然而,尽管存在数千种关联,但调节疾病风险的变异及其功能影响仍然很大程度上未知。这包括 SARS-CoV-2 感染,其后果从无症状到致命不等。通过全基因组关联研究鉴定出的大多数与 COVID-19 疾病相关的遗传风险变异位于非编码基因组中,可能通过改变疾病相关细胞和组织中的基因表达来发挥作用。为了大规模解决这个问题,我们使用自转录活性调节区测序(一种大规模并行报告分析)测试了超过 4800 个严重的 COVID-19 相关变异,以确定单个变异和变异组合对监管活动的影响。着眼于可能对肺产生影响的变异,在肺上皮细胞系 (A549) 中,我们鉴定了活性序列内的 166 个变异,其中 29 个特异性调节等位基因活性。通过评估变体组合,我们观察到对监管活动的加性和非加性影响。我们采用最先进的深度学习模型来解释等位基因特异性变异对调控活动和内源基因组特征的影响。我们的工作提供了一组优先的严重 COVID-19 相关变体,这些变体调节肺上皮细胞、候选转录因子和候选靶基因的调节活性,有可能改变疾病。

设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
为了大规模解决这个问题,我们使用自转录活性调节区测序(一种大规模并行报告分析)测试了超过 4800 个严重的 COVID-19 相关变异,以确定单个变异和变异组合对监管活动的影响。
主要结果
通过全基因组关联研究鉴定出的大多数与 COVID-19 疾病相关的遗传风险变异位于非编码基因组中,可能通过改变疾病相关细胞和组织中的基因表达来发挥作用。
解释与创新

局限与证据强度
通过评估变体组合,我们观察到对监管活动的加性和非加性影响。

Identifying severe COVID-19 risk variants modulating enhancer reporter activity in lung cells

Authors: Giovanna Weykopf, Wendy A. Bickmore, Simon C. Biddie, Elias T. Friman
Original Abstract
Common genetic variants contribute to risk for complex human diseases. However, despite thousands of associations, variants modulating disease risk and their functional impact remain largely unknown. This includes SARS-CoV-2 infection, where outcomes range from asymptomatic to fatal. Most genetic risk variants associated with COVID-19 disease, identified through genome wide association studies, are located in the non-coding genome and may function by altering gene expression in disease-relevant cells and tissues. To address this at scale, we tested >4800 severe COVID-19-associated variants to determine the impact of individual variants and variant combinations on regulatory activity using Self-Transcribing Active Regulatory Region sequencing, a massively-parallel reporter assay. Focusing on variants that may have their impact in the lung, in a lung epithelial cell line (A549) we identify 166 variants within active sequences, of which 29 modulate activity allele-specifically. Evaluating variant combinations, we observe both additive and non-additive effects on regulatory activity. We employ state-of-the-art deep learning models to interpret allele-specific variant effects on regulatory activity and endogenous genomic features. Our work provides a set of prioritised severe COVID-19-associated variants that modulate regulatory activity in lung epithelial cells, candidate transcription factors, and candidate target genes with potential to be disease modifying.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
However, despite thousands of associations, variants modulating disease risk and their functional impact remain largely unknown.

The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
To address this at scale, we tested >4800 severe COVID-19-associated variants to determine the impact of individual variants and variant combinations on regulatory activity using Self-Transcribing Active Regulatory Region sequencing, a massively-parallel reporter assay.
Main results
Most genetic risk variants associated with COVID-19 disease, identified through genome wide association studies, are located in the non-coding genome and may function by altering gene expression in disease-relevant cells and tissues.
Interpretation and novelty
We employ state-of-the-art deep learning models to interpret allele-specific variant effects on regulatory activity and endogenous genomic features.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our work provides a set of prioritised severe COVID-19-associated variants that modulate regulatory activity in lung epithelial cells, candidate transcription factors, and candidate target genes with potential to be disease modifying.
Limitations and evidence strength
Evaluating variant combinations, we observe both additive and non-additive effects on regulatory activity.

Covid-19 相关的超额死亡率:对选定国家的年龄分析

Covid-19 related excess mortality: An analysis by age for selected countries
作者： David Madden
摘要中文翻译背景:超额死亡率的衡量标准通常被认为是衡量各国在 2020-2022 年新冠大流行期间经历的最佳指标。然而,这些措施需要进行调整,以考虑到不同国家不同的年龄结构,因为与新冠病毒相关的死亡率可能因年龄而有很大差异。方法:本文使用短期死亡率波动数据集分析了一些国家的超额死亡率数据。将标准化年龄分布应用于数据以获得年龄调整的超额死亡率,并将其与粗略的超额死亡率数据进行比较。还针对同一数据集计算了生命损失年数 (YLL) 指标,并应用聚类分析来研究死亡率指标与人均 GDP 和不平等之间的关系。结果:年龄调整对一些国家有显着差异,但国家超额死亡率的总体排名变化很小,粗死亡率、年龄调整死亡率和YLL年龄调整超额死亡率指标的等级相关系数均超过0.95,与人均GDP的等级相关性为-0.69,但与基尼系数0.11的等级相关性不具有统计显着性。结论:新冠疫情期间死亡率过高的国家排名对于年龄调整和死亡率指标的选择来说是稳健的。聚类分析表明,具有相似结果的国家存在明显的聚类,特别是中欧和东欧的前计划经济体。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
超额死亡率被认为是评估各国在 Covid-19 大流行期间经历的最佳指标,但由于与年龄相关的死亡率不同,这些指标需要根据各国的年龄差异进行调整。本研究旨在分析和比较选定国家的年龄调整超额死亡率数据。
设计与对象
该研究使用短期死亡率波动数据集分析了一些国家的超额死亡率数据,应用标准化年龄分布来获得年龄调整后的超额死亡率,并将其与粗略的超额死亡率数据进行比较。
核心方法
该研究使用短期死亡率波动数据集,并应用标准化年龄分布来计算年龄调整的超额死亡率,然后与粗略的超额死亡率数据进行比较。此外,还计算了寿命损失年数 (YLL) 指标,并应用聚类分析来研究死亡率指标与人均 GDP 和不平等之间的关系。
主要结果
年龄调整对一些国家的超额死亡率排名产生显着影响,但总体排名变化很小,粗死亡率、年龄调整死亡率和 YLL 的排名相关系数超过 0.95。年龄调整超额死亡率与人均GDP的相关性为-0.69,但与基尼系数0.11的相关性并不具有统计显着性。
解释与创新
该研究发现,Covid-19期间死亡率过高的国家排名对于年龄调整和死亡率指标的选择来说是稳健的,具有相似结果的国家有明显的集群,特别是中欧和东欧的前计划经济体。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,按年龄调整的超额死亡率排名是各国在 Covid-19 大流行期间经历的可靠指标,可以为公共卫生战略和资源分配提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于选定的国家,并依赖于短期死亡率波动数据集,该数据集可能不完全具有代表性。由于仅摘要证据和使用的特定数据集,证据强度中等。

Covid-19 related excess mortality: An analysis by age for selected countries

Authors: David Madden
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Measures of excess mortality are generally regarded as providing the best metric for countries' experience during the Covid pandemic of 2020-2022. Such measures, however, need to be adjusted to take account of the different age structures of different countries as Covid-related mortality can differ substantially by age. METHODS: This paper analyses excess mortality data for a selection of countries using the Short Term Mortality Fluctuations dataset. A standardised age distribution is applied to the data to obtain age-adjusted excess mortality and this is compared to crude excess mortality data. The metric of years of life lost (YLL) is also calculated for the same dataset and cluster analysis is applied to investigate relationships between the mortality metrics and GDP per capita and inequality. RESULTS: The age adjustment makes a significant difference for some countries but the overall ranking of countries in terms of excess mortality changes very little, with rank correlation coefficients in excess of 0.95 for the metrics of crude mortality, age adjusted mortality and YLL Age adjusted excess mortality has a rank correlation of -0.69 with GDP per capita but the rank correlation with the Gini coefficient of 0.11 is not statistically significant. CONCLUSIONS: Rankings of countries by excess mortality during the Covid period are robust to age adjustment and choice of mortality metric. Cluster analysis suggests distinct clusterings of countries with similar outcomes, particularly the former planned economies of central and eastern Europe.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Excess mortality is considered the best metric for assessing countries' experiences during the Covid-19 pandemic, but these measures need to be adjusted for age differences across countries due to varying age-related mortality rates. This study aims to analyze and compare age-adjusted excess mortality data for selected countries.
Design and population
The study analyzes excess mortality data for a selection of countries using the Short Term Mortality Fluctuations dataset, applying a standardised age distribution to obtain age-adjusted excess mortality and comparing it to crude excess mortality data.
Core methods
The study uses the Short Term Mortality Fluctuations dataset and applies a standardised age distribution to calculate age-adjusted excess mortality, which is then compared to crude excess mortality data. Additionally, the metric of years of life lost (YLL) is calculated, and cluster analysis is applied to investigate relationships between mortality metrics and GDP per capita and inequality.
Main results
The age adjustment significantly affects excess mortality rankings for some countries, but overall rankings change very little, with rank correlation coefficients exceeding 0.95 for crude mortality, age-adjusted mortality, and YLL. Age-adjusted excess mortality has a rank correlation of -0.69 with GDP per capita, but the correlation with the Gini coefficient of 0.11 is not statistically significant.
Interpretation and novelty
The study finds that rankings of countries by excess mortality during the Covid-19 period are robust to age adjustment and choice of mortality metric, with distinct clusterings of countries with similar outcomes, particularly former planned economies of central and eastern Europe.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that age-adjusted excess mortality rankings are reliable indicators of countries' experiences during the Covid-19 pandemic, which can inform public health strategies and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a selection of countries and relies on the Short Term Mortality Fluctuations dataset, which may not be fully representative. The evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only evidence and the specific dataset used.

COVID-19 后患者的神经认知和心理症状 (PASC24):前瞻性队列研究方案。

Neurocognitive and psychological symptoms in post COVID-19 patients (PASC24): prospective cohort study protocol.
作者： Adrian Meehan, Zbigniew Dzialanski, Hans Hjelmqvist, Per Julin, Per Thunberg, Yvonne Freund-Levi
摘要中文翻译简介:关于 COVID-19 的后期影响或所谓的 COVID 急性后遗症 (PASC) 及其发病机制的知识仍然非常有限。这项观察性研究的目的是绘制 COVID-19 感染对晚期神经认知的影响。目的是增加对潜在生物过程的了解,并描述生物标志物如何与症状程度相关,随着时间的推移(24 个月)绘制它们作为诊断标志物的作用。方法和分析:这项前瞻性观察性队列研究是纵向的,监测感染 COVID-19 后出现神经认知和心理症状的患者,以分析其对大脑的影响。为此,我们引入了数字神经心理学平台(Mindmore)。我们还旨在分析血液和脑脊液中的潜在诊断和预后标志物,并使用大脑 MRI。我们将衡量患者恢复工作和学习的情况以及对其 ADL 功能和生活质量的影响。正在进行的研究旨在对 100 名接受 PASC 的研究参与者进行为期 24 个月的随访,分 3 次访视:基线、12 个月和 24 个月。该研究的结果可以作为未来医疗保健政策管理的指导,并可能带来可能的治愈或缓解症状的治疗方法。伦理与传播:本研究得到瑞典伦理审查局批准(日记号:2020-0604(主申请)、2021-03205、2023-00023-02、2024-00426-02(修正案))。参与者将被要求提供知情同意。这项研究的结果将在同行评审期刊上报道并在科学会议上展示。试用注册号:NCT06298006。

问题与背景
这项观察性研究的目的是绘制 COVID-19 感染对晚期神经认知的影响。
设计与对象
参与者将被要求提供知情同意。
核心方法
方法和分析:这项前瞻性观察性队列研究是纵向的,监测感染 COVID-19 后出现神经认知和心理症状的患者,以分析其对大脑的影响。
主要结果
这项研究的结果将在同行评审期刊上报道并在科学会议上展示。
解释与创新
所提供的证据没有清楚地区分作者的解释或研究的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究的结果可以作为未来医疗保健政策管理的指导,并可能带来可能的治愈或缓解症状的治疗方法。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Neurocognitive and psychological symptoms in post COVID-19 patients (PASC24): prospective cohort study protocol.

Authors: Adrian Meehan, Zbigniew Dzialanski, Hans Hjelmqvist, Per Julin, Per Thunberg, Yvonne Freund-Levi
Original Abstract
INTRODUCTION: Knowledge about the late effects of COVID-19, or so-called post-acute sequelae of COVID (PASC), and its pathogenesis is still very limited. The purpose of this observational study is to chart late neurocognitive effects of the COVID-19 infection. The aim is to increase knowledge of the underlying biological processes and describe how biomarkers correlate with degree of symptoms, mapping their role as diagnostic markers over time (24 months). METHODS AND ANALYSIS: This prospective observational cohort study is longitudinal and monitors patients with neurocognitive and psychological symptoms after COVID-19 infection in order to analyse the impact on the brain. For these purposes, we introduce a digital neuropsychological platform (Mindmore). We also aim to analyse potential diagnostic and prognostic markers from blood and cerebrospinal fluid and use MRI of the brain. We will measure the patients' return to work and studies as well as any impact on their ADL functions and quality of life. The ongoing study aims to follow-up to 100 study participants with PASC for 24 months at three visits: baseline, 12 months and 24 months. The findings of the study can be used as guidance for future policy management in healthcare and may lead to a possible curative or symptom-relieving treatment. ETHICS AND DISSEMINATION: This study was approved by the Swedish Ethical Review Authority (diary number: 2020-0604 (main application), 2021-03205, 2023-00023-02, 2024-00426-02 (amendments)). Participants will be required to provide informed consent. The results of this study will be reported in peer-reviewed journals and presented at scientific conferences. TRIAL REGISTRATION NUMBER: NCT06298006.

covid-19 大流行期间肠道病毒的环境流行率:一项为期三年的监测研究。

Environmental prevalence of enteric viruses across the covid-19 pandemic: A three-year monitoring study.
作者： Francesco Serra, I. Di Bartolo, Martina Levante, D. Iaccarino, F. Di Nocera, M. Monini, G. Ianiro, Rosario Carbone, F. D'Apice, L. De Maio
摘要中文翻译肠道病毒持续以高浓度从人类粪便中排出,并通过经过处理和未经处理的废水传播到水生环境中,其中大部分最终排入沿海海水。因此,对这些病原体的环境监测可以反映它们在人群中的传播情况,并作为新病毒或变种出现以及现有病毒传播增加的预警工具。在这项研究中,我们调查了坎帕尼亚地区(意大利南部)沿海水域中主要胃肠病毒(腺病毒、诺如病毒、轮状病毒)以及具有不同临床相关性的其他肠道病毒的发生和分子多样性。根据季节、采样年份和废水排放类型评估病毒流行率。总共收集了 500 个海水样本,其中近一半 (44.0%) 的至少一种病毒目标检测呈阳性。病毒检出率多年来有所不同,可能是受到与 COVID-19 大流行相关的流行病学动态的影响。值得注意的是,系统发育分析表明,大多数环境病毒序列与先前在同一地理区域的人类临床病例中鉴定的毒株相同或密切相关,这支持了人类感染与环境污染之间的强大流行病学联系。总体而言,这些发现强调了在“同一个健康”框架内进行综合环境监测对公共卫生风险评估的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肠道病毒被广泛排泄并排放到水生环境中,但它们在沿海水域的循环和多样性仍未完全表征,这为及早发现新出现或死灰复燃的病毒威胁造成了监测缺口。
设计与对象
一项为期三年的环境监测研究从坎帕尼亚地区(意大利南部)沿海水域收集了 500 个海水样本,以评估病毒的发生和多样性。
核心方法
对海水样本进行了主要胃肠病毒(腺病毒、诺如病毒、轮状病毒)和其他肠道病毒的分析,并按季节、采样年份和废水排放类型评估了病毒流行情况。
主要结果
500 个海水样本中近一半 (44.0%) 的至少一种病毒目标检测呈阳性,系统发育分析表明,大多数环境病毒序列与先前在同一地区的人类临床病例中发现的毒株相同或密切相关。
解释与创新
该研究表明,沿海水域的环境监测可以反映人类病毒传播情况,并强调人类感染与环境污染之间的流行病学联系,支持其作为预警工具的使用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持将环境监测纳入公共卫生风险评估框架,以监测病毒传播,并在“同一个健康”方法中及早发现新出现或死灰复燃的病毒威胁。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于意大利单一地区的沿海水域,并且缺乏详细的时间或空间控制,这限制了推论的普遍性和强度。

Environmental prevalence of enteric viruses across the covid-19 pandemic: A three-year monitoring study.

Authors: Francesco Serra, I. Di Bartolo, Martina Levante, D. Iaccarino, F. Di Nocera, M. Monini, G. Ianiro, Rosario Carbone, F. D'Apice, L. De Maio
Original Abstract
Enteric viruses are continuously excreted in high concentrations in human feces and are disseminated into aquatic environments through treated and untreated wastewater, much of which is ultimately discharged into coastal marine waters. Environmental surveillance of these pathogens may therefore reflect their circulation in human populations and serve as an early-warning tool for the emergence of new viruses or variants, as well as increased transmission of existing ones. In this study, we investigated the occurrence and molecular diversity of major gastroenteric viruses - adenovirus, noroviruses, rotavirus - together with other enteric viruses with variable clinical relevance, in coastal marine waters of Campania Region (Southern Italy). Viral prevalence was evaluated according to season, sampling year, and type of wastewater discharge. A total of 500 marine water samples were collected, of which nearly half (44.0%) tested positive for at least one viral target. Viral detection rates varied across years, presumably influenced by the epidemiological dynamics associated with the COVID-19 pandemic. Notably, phylogenetic analyses revealed that most environmental viral sequences were identical or closely related to strains previously identified in human clinical cases from the same geographical area, supporting a strong epidemiological link between human infections and environmental contamination. Overall, these findings underscore the value of integrated environmental surveillance for public health risk assessment within a One Health framework.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Enteric viruses are widely excreted and discharged into aquatic environments, yet their circulation and diversity in coastal waters remain incompletely characterized, creating a surveillance gap for early detection of emerging or resurgent viral threats.
Design and population
A three-year environmental monitoring study collected 500 marine water samples from coastal waters of Campania Region (Southern Italy) to assess viral occurrence and diversity.
Core methods
Marine water samples were analyzed for major gastroenteric viruses (adenovirus, noroviruses, rotavirus) and other enteric viruses, with viral prevalence evaluated by season, sampling year, and type of wastewater discharge.
Main results
Nearly half (44.0%) of the 500 marine water samples tested positive for at least one viral target, and phylogenetic analyses showed that most environmental viral sequences were identical or closely related to strains previously identified in human clinical cases from the same area.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that environmental surveillance of coastal waters can reflect human viral circulation and highlights the epidemiological link between human infections and environmental contamination, supporting its use as an early-warning tool.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the integration of environmental surveillance into public health risk assessment frameworks to monitor viral circulation and inform early detection of emerging or resurgent viral threats within a One Health approach.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on coastal marine waters in a single Italian region and the absence of detailed temporal or spatial controls, which constrains generalizability and strength of inference.

Nirmatrelvir 用于治疗急性 COVID-19,以预防长期 COVID(挪威全景):一项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验。

Nirmatrelvir for acute COVID-19 to prevent long COVID (PANORAMIC Norway): a double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial.
作者： Oddvar Oppegaard, Bjørn Blomberg, Rebecca Jane Cox, Arild Iversen, Nikolai Norevik Myklebust, Marte Glambek, Camilla Tøndel, Tuva Børresdatter Dahl, Tori Friis, Elise Mjøs
摘要中文翻译背景:急性 COVID-19 后长期症状很常见,特别是疲劳、认知问题和呼吸困难。队列研究表明,急性 COVID-19 的抗病毒治疗可能会预防 COVID-19 后病情(也称为长期 COVID)的发展,但抗病毒药物的疗效尚未在前瞻性研究中得到验证。我们的目的是研究用尼马曲韦-利托那韦治疗急性 SARS-CoV-2 感染是否会降低长期感染新冠病毒的风险。方法:在这项双盲、随机、安慰剂对照试验中,参与者是从挪威三个地点(卑尔根、奥斯陆和奥勒松)的市卫生保健服务机构招募的。年龄在 18-65 岁、经 PCR 或侧流检测证实感染 SARS-CoV-2 且症状持续 5 天或更短的非住院成年人有资格纳入。主要排除标准包括妊娠或哺乳、慢性肾功能不全或慢性肝功能障碍,以及研究者判断因住院或死亡风险增加而需要尼马曲韦-利托那韦治疗的任何人。参与者按照 1:1 的比例分配,接受口服 300 mg nirmatrelvir 和 100 mg ritonavir 或安慰剂,每天两次,持续 5 天,使用预先生成的随机列表,无需分层或区组调整。参与者、临床医生和研究团队对治疗分配情况不知情。主要结局是长期新冠肺炎,定义为患者在 3 个月随访时报告的疲劳、呼吸困难和/或认知症状。安全性作为次要结果进行分析,包括不良事件、住院和死亡。主要结局和安全性均在意向治疗人群中进行了评估。该试验已在 ClinicalTrials.gov (NCT05852873) 注册,现已不对新参与者开放。调查结果:2023 年 5 月 12 日至 2025 年 6 月 11 日期间,我们招募了 144 名参与者,其中 66 名被分配到尼尔马瑞韦-利托那韦组,78 名被分配到安慰剂组。在方案中,我们计划招募 2000 名参与者,但由于招募不足,试验被指导委员会提前停止。在完成随访的 143 名参与者中,与安慰剂组(77 人中的 33 人 [43%])相比,尼尔马韦-利托那韦组(66 人中的 17 人 [26%])在 3 个月时长期感染新冠病毒的风险显着降低,相当于安慰剂组中单个缺失值数据插补后的相对风险为 0·60(95% CI 0·37-0·98;p=0·039)。在尼尔马瑞韦-利托那韦组中,有 5 名患者因不良事件而停止治疗。尼马曲韦-利托那韦组最常见的不良事件是味觉或嗅觉变化(尼马曲韦-利托那韦组 66 例中有 57 例 [86%],安慰剂组 78 例中有 14 例 [18%])和恶心或呕吐(19 例 [29%] 比 8 例 [10%])。相反,心悸在安慰剂组(78 人中的 10 人 [13%])比尼马瑞韦-利托那韦组(66 人中的 2 人 [3%])更常见。没有报告严重不良事件。解释:在 3 个月的随访中,用尼马曲韦-利托那韦治疗急性 COVID-19 与长期感染 COVID 的风险显着降低相关。有限的样本量无法得出确切的结论,需要进一步的临床试验。资金来源:国家卫生当局的 KlinBeForsk 计划、挪威西部地区卫生局、Helse Møre og Romsdal 医院信托基金、流感中心、Haukeland 大学医院和挪威卑尔根卑尔根大学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们的目的是研究用尼马曲韦-利托那韦治疗急性 SARS-CoV-2 感染是否会降低长期感染新冠病毒的风险。
设计与对象
参与者按照 1:1 的比例分配,接受口服 300 mg nirmatrelvir 和 100 mg ritonavir 或安慰剂,每天两次,持续 5 天,使用预先生成的随机列表,无需分层或区组调整。
核心方法
安全性作为次要结果进行分析,包括不良事件、住院和死亡。
主要结果
在完成随访的 143 名参与者中,与安慰剂组(77 人中的 33 人 [43%])相比,尼尔马韦-利托那韦组(66 人中的 17 人 [26%])在 3 个月时长期感染新冠病毒的风险显着降低,相当于安慰剂组中单个缺失值数据插补后的相对风险为 0·60(95% CI 0·37-0·98;p=0·039)。
解释与创新
相反,心悸在安慰剂组(78 人中的 10 人 [13%])比尼马瑞韦-利托那韦组(66 人中的 2 人 [3%])更常见。
科研与公卫意义
在 3 个月的随访中,使用尼马瑞韦-利托那韦治疗急性 COVID-19 与长期感染 COVID 的风险显着降低相关。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Nirmatrelvir for acute COVID-19 to prevent long COVID (PANORAMIC Norway): a double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial.

Authors: Oddvar Oppegaard, Bjørn Blomberg, Rebecca Jane Cox, Arild Iversen, Nikolai Norevik Myklebust, Marte Glambek, Camilla Tøndel, Tuva Børresdatter Dahl, Tori Friis, Elise Mjøs
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Long-term symptoms are common after acute COVID-19, particularly fatigue, cognitive problems, and dyspnoea. Cohort studies have suggested that antiviral treatment of acute COVID-19 might prevent development of post-COVID-19 condition (also known as long COVID), but the efficacy of antivirals has yet to be verified in prospective studies. We aimed to investigate whether treatment of acute SARS-CoV-2 infection with nirmatrelvir-ritonavir would reduce the risk of long COVID. METHODS: In this double-blind, randomised, placebo-controlled trial, participants were recruited from the municipal health-care service at three sites in Norway (Bergen, Oslo, and Ålesund). Non-hospitalised adults aged 18-65 years with SARS-CoV-2 infection confirmed by PCR or lateral flow test and symptoms for 5 days or fewer were eligible for inclusion. Key exclusion criteria included pregnancy or lactation, chronic renal impairment or chronic liver dysfunction, and any person judged by the investigator to need nirmatrelvir-ritonavir treatment due to increased risk of hospitalisation or death. Participants were allocated in a 1:1 ratio to receive oral 300 mg nirmatrelvir and 100 mg ritonavir, or placebo, twice a day for 5 days using a pre-generated randomisation list without stratification or block adjustment. Participants, clinicians, and the study team were masked to treatment allocation. The primary outcome was long COVID, defined as patient-reported fatigue, dyspnoea, and/or cognitive symptoms at 3 months' follow-up. Safety was analysed as a secondary outcome, and included adverse events, hospital admissions, and deaths. Both the primary outcome and safety were assessed in the intention-to-treat population. The trial is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov (NCT05852873) and is now closed to new participants. FINDINGS: Between May 12, 2023, and June 11, 2025, we enrolled 144 participants, of whom 66 were assigned to nirmatrelvir-ritonavir and 78 to placebo. In the protocol we planned to enrol 2000 participants, but the trial was stopped prematurely by the steering committee due to insufficient recruitment. Among the 143 participants who completed follow-up, the risk of long COVID at 3 months was significantly reduced in the nirmatrelvir-ritonavir group (17 [26%] of 66) compared with the placebo group (33 [43%] of 77), corresponding to a relative risk after imputation of data for the single missing value in the placebo group of 0·60 (95% CI 0·37-0·98; p=0·039). In the nirmatrelvir-ritonavir group, five patients discontinued treatment due to adverse events. The most common adverse events in the nirmatrelvir-ritonavir group were change in taste or smell (57 [86%] of 66 in the nirmatrelvir-ritonavir group vs 14 [18%] of 78 in the placebo group) and nausea or vomiting (19 [29%] vs eight [10%]). Inversely, palpitations were more common in the placebo group (ten [13%] of 78) than in the nirmatrelvir-ritonavir group (two [3%] of 66). No severe adverse events were reported. INTERPRETATION: Treatment with nirmatrelvir-ritonavir for acute COVID-19 was associated with a significant reduction in the risk of long COVID at 3 months' follow-up. The limited sample size precludes firm conclusions, and further clinical trials are warranted. FUNDING: National Health Authorities' KlinBeForsk programme, Western Norway Regional Health Authority, Helse Møre og Romsdal Hospital Trust, and The Influenza Centre, Haukeland University Hospital and University of Bergen, Bergen, Norway.

纳米孔介导的组装能够精确、连续地合成用于 COVID-19 疫苗的 mRNA 封装的脂质纳米颗粒。

Nanopore-Mediated Assembly Enables Precise and Continuous Synthesis of mRNA-Encapsulated Lipid Nanoparticles for COVID-19 Vaccines.
作者： Zhixiang Liu, William Stewart, Yilong Teng, Yufeng Song, Maoping Tang, Yuxuan Guo, Xue-Qing Zhang, Sirkar KK, Xiaoyang Xu
摘要中文翻译RNA 疗法正在重塑现代医学,COVID-19 大流行期间基于脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 的 mRNA 疫苗的快速部署就是例证。然而,大规模、可重复地生成具有精确定义的理化性质的 LNP 的能力仍然是一个严峻的挑战,特别是当颗粒尺寸和尺寸分布强烈影响生物分布、细胞摄取和治疗效果时。传统的混合技术可确保再现性,但对界面混合和纳米粒子组装的控制有限,限制了可调谐性和可扩展性。我们提出了一种基于中空纤维膜 (HFM) 的平台,该平台利用密集的纳米级孔阵列来介导均匀、高度局部的界面混合,从而实现受控的脂质自组装和 RNA 封装。这种纳米孔介导的结构允许连续、高通量合成具有可调粒径、窄粒径分布和高封装效率的 LNP,且颗粒特性与膜孔径直接相关。HFM 衍生的 LNP 表现出强大的体外转染和有效的体内免疫反应,并且与多种脂质化学和核酸有效负载(包括 mRNA 疫苗构建体)兼容。总之,这项工作将基于 HFM 的纳米孔组装确立为一种通用且可扩展的方法,用于生产明确的 LNP,与当前和未来的 mRNA 疫苗和治疗开发直接相关。

问题与背景
RNA 疗法正在重塑现代医学,COVID-19 大流行期间基于脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 的 mRNA 疫苗的快速部署就是例证。

提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
提供的证据没有明确说明直接观察到的主要结果。

Nanopore-Mediated Assembly Enables Precise and Continuous Synthesis of mRNA-Encapsulated Lipid Nanoparticles for COVID-19 Vaccines.

Authors: Zhixiang Liu, William Stewart, Yilong Teng, Yufeng Song, Maoping Tang, Yuxuan Guo, Xue-Qing Zhang, Sirkar KK, Xiaoyang Xu
Original Abstract
RNA therapeutics are reshaping modern medicine, as exemplified by the rapid deployment of lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-based mRNA vaccines during the COVID-19 pandemic. However, the ability to reproducibly generate LNPs with precisely defined physicochemical properties at scale remains a critical challenge, particularly as the particle size and size distribution strongly influence biodistribution, cellular uptake, and therapeutic efficacy. Conventional mixing technologies ensure reproducibility but offer limited control over interfacial mixing and nanoparticle assembly, constraining both tunability and scalability. We present a hollow fiber membrane (HFM)-based platform that leverages dense arrays of nanoscale pores to mediate uniform, highly localized interfacial mixing, enabling controlled lipid self-assembly and RNA encapsulation. This nanopore-mediated architecture allows continuous, high-throughput synthesis of LNPs with tunable particle sizes, narrow size distributions, and high encapsulation efficiencies, with particle characteristics directly correlated to the membrane pore size. HFM-derived LNPs exhibit robust in vitro transfection and potent in vivo immune responses and are compatible with multiple lipid chemistries and nucleic acid payloads, including mRNA vaccine constructs. Together, this work establishes HFM-based nanopore assembly as a versatile and scalable approach for producing well-defined LNPs, with direct relevance to the current and future mRNA vaccine and therapeutic development.

Question and background
RNA therapeutics are reshaping modern medicine, as exemplified by the rapid deployment of lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-based mRNA vaccines during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.

HFM-derived LNPs exhibit robust in vitro transfection and potent in vivo immune responses and are compatible with multiple lipid chemistries and nucleic acid payloads, including mRNA vaccine constructs.
Scientific and public-health significance
Conventional mixing technologies ensure reproducibility but offer limited control over interfacial mixing and nanoparticle assembly, constraining both tunability and scalability.
Limitations and evidence strength
Together, this work establishes HFM-based nanopore assembly as a versatile and scalable approach for producing well-defined LNPs, with direct relevance to the current and future mRNA vaccine and therapeutic development.

英国按疫苗接种状态和变异划分的 SARS-CoV-2 感染的血清学结果。

Serological outcomes of SARS-CoV-2 infection by vaccination status and variant in England.
作者： Catherine Quinot, Rachel Lunt, Freja Kirsebom, Nick Andrews, Heather Whitaker, Catriona Skarnes, Louise Letley, Donna Haskins, Catriona Angel, Skye Firminger
摘要中文翻译背景:在整个SARS-CoV-2大流行期间,多种疫苗已投入使用,英格兰出现了具有不同严重程度和免疫特征的变异株。利用疫苗符合条件人群的COVID-19增强监测数据,我们根据疫苗接种状况和变异株研究了SARS-CoV-2感染后的抗体反应。方法:2021年2月至2022年4月期间,通过英格兰社区PCR检测数据确定了SARS-CoV-2 PCR阳性且符合条件的人群,由护士联系他们在招募时和招募后21天完成问卷。参与者收到自采样试剂盒,并在招募后第1天、第3天和第7天采集自采样鼻咽拭子,以及急性(第1天)、恢复期(随访)血清和唾液样本。回归分析用于研究N抗体血清转化如何因疫苗接种状况而异,以及N和S抗体水平如何因疫苗接种状况而异,并按变异株分层。区间截尾分析和回归分析用于研究急性S抗体水平对阳性持续时间、循环阈值值、自我报告的症状严重程度和报告症状数量的影响。结果:共纳入1,497名PCR阳性个体。其中369名(24.7%)未接种疫苗,359名(24.0%)感染了Alpha变异株,762名(50.9%)感染了Delta变异株,376名(25.2%)感染了Omicron变异株。参与者的中位年龄为49岁(IQR 39-57)。恢复期抗N抗体水平在接种疫苗的个体中较低,而恢复期抗S抗体水平在接种疫苗的个体中较高,并随接种剂量增加而增加。急性抗S抗体水平随接种剂量增加而增加。较高的急性抗S抗体水平与较短的阳性持续时间相关(总体和针对Delta变异株)。较高的急性抗S抗体水平也与较高的Ct值相关(总体和针对Alpha和Delta变异株)。急性抗S抗体水平与自我报告的症状严重程度之间没有关联。急性抗S抗体水平较高的个体报告6种或更多症状的可能性较小(总体和针对Delta变异株)。结论:了解抗体反应的特征、其随时间的变化以及其提供的免疫力,对于指导未来的疫苗接种策略和政策至关重要。我们的发现表明,疫苗接种与高急性抗S抗体水平相关,但恢复期抗N抗体水平较低。高抗S抗体水平与感染持续时间缩短、传染性降低相关,并且可能也与症状严重程度和症状数量减少相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究满足了公共卫生需求,了解英国感染 SARS-CoV-2 疫苗接种和新出现的变体如何影响感染后的抗体反应,英国部署了多种疫苗,并且传播了具有不同免疫特征的变体。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性强化监测研究于 2021 年 2 月至 2022 年 4 月期间在英格兰招募了 PCR 阳性、符合疫苗资格的个体,在多个时间点收集急性和恢复期血清、口腔液和自检拭子样本。
核心方法
参与者在招募时和 21 天后完成了调查问卷;回归和区间删失分析用于根据疫苗接种状态和变异比较 N 和 S 抗体水平,并将急性 S 抗体水平与阳性持续时间、Ct 值和症状概况联系起来。
主要结果
在1,497名PCR阳性个体(中位年龄49岁)中,24.7%未接种疫苗;24.0%感染了Alpha变异株,50.9%感染了Delta变异株,25.2%感染了Omicron变异株。接种疫苗的个体恢复期抗N水平较低,而恢复期和急性抗S水平随疫苗剂量增加而上升。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗接种会增加急性抗 S 抗体水平并降低恢复期抗 N 水平,而较高的急性抗 S 水平会缩短感染持续时间,并可能降低传染性和症状负担,这凸显了对变异特异性免疫的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过显示先前的疫苗接种如何调节感染后的抗体动力学和临床结果,为加强接种时机和变异靶向疫苗的决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察设计以及对自我报告症状和自我采样的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差;鉴于抽样框架稳健,但缺乏随机性和潜在的无法测量的混杂因素,证据被评估为中等。

Serological outcomes of SARS-CoV-2 infection by vaccination status and variant in England.

Authors: Catherine Quinot, Rachel Lunt, Freja Kirsebom, Nick Andrews, Heather Whitaker, Catriona Skarnes, Louise Letley, Donna Haskins, Catriona Angel, Skye Firminger
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Throughout the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic, several vaccines have been rolled out and distinct variants with different severity and immune profiles emerged in England. Using data from enhanced surveillance of COVID-19 in vaccine eligible individuals we investigated the antibody response following SARS-CoV-2 infection according to vaccination status and variant. METHODS: SARS-CoV-2 PCR-positive eligible individuals were identified from community PCR testing data in England between February 2021 and April 2022 and contacted by nurses to complete questionnaires at recruitment and 21 days post recruitment. Individuals were sent self-sampling kits and self-sampled nasal/oropharyngeal swabs were taken day 1, day 3 and day 7 post-recruitment as well as acute (day 1), convalescent (follow-up) serum and oral fluid samples. Regression analyses were used to investigate how N antibody seroconversion differs by vaccine status, and to investigate how N and S antibody levels differ by vaccine status overall and stratified by variants. Interval-censored analyses and regression analyses were used to investigate the effect of acute S antibody levels on the duration of positivity, the cycle threshold values, the self-reported symptom severity and the number of symptoms reported. RESULTS: A total of 1,497 PCR positive individuals were included. A total of 369 (24.7%) individuals were unvaccinated, 359 (24.0%) participants were infected with Alpha, 762 (50.9%) with Delta and 376 (25.2%) with Omicron. The median age of participants was 49 years old (IQR 39-57). Convalescent anti-N antibody levels were lower in vaccinated individuals and convalescent anti-S antibody levels were higher in vaccinated individuals and increased with the number of doses received. Acute anti-S antibody level increased with the number of doses received. Higher acute anti-S antibody levels were associated with a shorter duration of positivity (overall and for the Delta variant). Higher acute anti-S antibody levels were also associated with higher Ct values (overall and for the Alpha and Delta variants). There was no association between the acute anti-S antibody level and self-reported symptom severity. Individuals with higher acute anti-S antibody level were less likely to report six or more symptoms (overall and for Delta variant). CONCLUSION: Understanding the characteristics of the antibody response, its dynamics over time and the immunity it confers is important to inform future vaccination strategies and policies. Our findings suggest that vaccination is associated with high acute anti-S antibody level but reduced convalescent anti-N antibody level. High anti-S antibody level is associated with reduced duration of infection, reduced infectiousness and may also be associated with reduced symptoms severity and number of symptoms.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to understand how SARS-CoV-2 vaccination and emerging variants shaped antibody responses after infection in England, where multiple vaccines were deployed and variants with distinct immune profiles circulated.
Design and population
A prospective enhanced surveillance study recruited PCR-positive, vaccine-eligible individuals in England between February 2021 and April 2022, collecting acute and convalescent sera, oral fluid, and self-sampled swabs at multiple time points.
Core methods
Participants completed questionnaires at recruitment and 21 days later; regression and interval-censored analyses were used to compare N and S antibody levels by vaccination status and variant, and to relate acute S antibody levels to duration of positivity, Ct values, and symptom profiles.
Main results
Among 1,497 PCR-positive individuals (median age 49 years), 24.7% were unvaccinated; 24.0% were infected with Alpha, 50.9% with Delta, and 25.2% with Omicron. Convalescent anti-N levels were lower in vaccinated individuals, while convalescent and acute anti-S levels rose with the number of vaccine doses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that vaccination increases acute anti-S antibody levels and reduces convalescent anti-N levels, and that higher acute anti-S levels shorten infection duration and may lower infectiousness and symptom burden, highlighting implications for variant-specific immunity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform decisions on booster timing and variant-targeted vaccines by showing how prior vaccination modulates antibody kinetics and clinical outcomes after infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and reliance on self-reported symptoms and self-sampling, which may introduce bias; the evidence is assessed as moderate given the robust sampling framework but lack of randomization and potential unmeasured confounding.

描述马拉维卫生部在 COVID-19 大流行期间部署的国家聊天机器人:回顾性数据分析。

Describing a National Chatbot Deployed by the Ministry of Health in Malawi During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Retrospective Data Analysis.
作者： Isaach Ndemera, Yu-Ting Sunny Hsieh, Kingston Chirwa, Selemani Ngwira, Alanna Denny, Tsung Shu Joseph Wu, Hsin-Yi Lee, Griphin Baxter Chirambo, Simeon Luka Yosefe, Ben Chilima
摘要中文翻译背景:马拉维是非洲国家中率先实施协调一致、政府主导的努力,利用数字医疗工具简化 COVID-19 支持的工作。为了应对这一流行病,我们开发了一款 COVID-19 WhatsApp 聊天机器人,以在流行病期间为公众提供信息、症状报告和服务导航支持。目的:本研究描述了马拉维 COVID-19 大流行期间 WhatsApp 聊天机器人的全国部署、功能和使用模式。方法:对 2020 年 5 月至 2023 年 5 月聊天机器人交互数据进行回顾性描述性分析。使用描述性统计总结了用户对关键聊天机器人功能的参与度,并使用时间序列分析将报告的 COVID-19 病例趋势与聊天机器人、紧急操作呼叫中心和 Chipatala cha pa Foni(一项国家热线倡议)的访问模式进行比较。结果:聊天机器人被访问了 347,117 次,其中 70.8% (n=245,895) 的经过验证的 WhatsApp 访问主要针对 COVID-19 统计数据。聊天机器人的使用模式显示出与 COVID-19 病例趋势的时间一致性,特别是在第一波和第二波大流行期间,以及在封锁事件后观察到的增加。在整个大流行期间,聊天机器人停机的天数为 38% (407/1070),其中最长的一段时间与 2021 年全国疫苗推出时间一致,在此期间引入了与疫苗相关的功能。此次扩展后,聊天机器人记录了 198 条与 COVID-19 疫苗相关的谣言和 644 条疫苗常见问题的访问次数。与基于呼叫的服务相比,聊天机器人记录了更高的整体交互,而通过呼叫中心平台更频繁地报告症状。结论:本研究描述了马拉维全国性 COVID-19 WhatsApp 聊天机器人的开发和使用。研究结果强调了信息寻求行为的模式、功能使用的变化以及系统可用性对参与度的影响。这些见解可以为类似环境中数字健康传播工具的设计、实施和可持续性提供参考。

Describing a National Chatbot Deployed by the Ministry of Health in Malawi During the COVID-19 Pandemic: Retrospective Data Analysis.

Authors: Isaach Ndemera, Yu-Ting Sunny Hsieh, Kingston Chirwa, Selemani Ngwira, Alanna Denny, Tsung Shu Joseph Wu, Hsin-Yi Lee, Griphin Baxter Chirambo, Simeon Luka Yosefe, Ben Chilima
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Malawi was a pioneer among African countries in implementing a coordinated, government-led effort to streamline COVID-19 support using digital health tools. In response to the pandemic, a COVID-19 WhatsApp chatbot was developed to support the public with information, symptom reporting, and service navigation during the pandemic. OBJECTIVE: This study describes the national deployment, functionality, and use patterns of the WhatsApp chatbot during the COVID-19 pandemic in Malawi. METHODS: A retrospective descriptive analysis of chatbot interaction data from May 2020 to May 2023 was conducted. User engagement with key chatbot functions was summarized using descriptive statistics, and time-series analysis was used to compare trends in reported COVID-19 cases with access patterns to the chatbot, the emergency operation call center, and Chipatala cha pa Foni (a national hotline initiative). RESULTS: The chatbot was accessed 347,117 times, with 70.8% (n=245,895) of validated WhatsApp accesses focused on COVID-19 statistics. Chatbot use patterns showed temporal alignment with COVID-19 case trends, particularly during the first and second pandemic waves and increases observed after lockdown events. Throughout the pandemic, chatbot downtime occurred in 38% (407/1070) of days, with the most prolonged period coinciding with the national vaccine rollout in 2021, during which vaccine-related functionalities were introduced. Following this expansion, the chatbot recorded 198 COVID-19 vaccine-related rumors and 644 accesses to vaccine frequently asked questions. Compared with call-based services, the chatbot recorded higher overall interactions, whereas symptoms were more frequently reported through call center platforms. CONCLUSIONS: This study provides a descriptive account of the development and use of a national COVID-19 WhatsApp chatbot in Malawi. The findings highlight patterns of information-seeking behavior, variation in feature use, and the influence of system availability on engagement. These insights may inform the design, implementation, and sustainability of digital health communication tools in similar settings.

Design and population

Limitations and evidence strength

对 SARS-CoV-2 受体结合域保守表位特异性肽进行生物淘选和纳米酶辅助双肽夹心 ELISA,用于超灵敏检测 SARS-CoV-2 抗原

Biopanning of Peptides Specific to the Conserved Epitope of the SARS-CoV-2 Receptor-Binding Domain and Nanozyme-Assisted Dual Peptide-Based Sandwich ELISA for Ultrasensitive Detection of the SARS-CoV-2 Antigen
作者： Mingyang Wang, Haipeng Yu, Wanjian Liu, Jinlong Yuan, Zihan Bian, Yuhan Chen, Aihua Liu
摘要中文翻译作为SARS-CoV-2刺突蛋白(SARS-CoV-2 SP)的亚基,受体结合域(RBD)可以特异性结合人血管紧张素转换酶2(ACE2),从而实现病毒感染宿主细胞。因此,探索能够以良好亲和力与SARS-CoV-2 RBD保守表位结合的探针,从而识别各种SARS-CoV-2变异体,用于诊断、疫苗接种和开发新的检测方法具有极其重要的意义。在此,我们通过噬菌体展示对肽 RN3(序列:YSIDWVFHHPML)进行了生物淘选,它可以以优异的亲和力和选择性与 SARS-CoV-2 RBD 结合。分子动力学模拟和分子对接验证,肽RN3中的His8和Met11残基通过氢键与SARS-CoV-2 SP上的SARS-CoV-2 RBD亚基保守表位的Asn487和Tyr489残基(分别为His8-Asn487和Met11-Tyr489)结合。丙氨酸扫描证实肽 RN3 的 Met11 是与 SARS-CoV-2 SP 结合的关键氨基酸。抑制 SARS-CoV-2 SP 与 ACE2 结合的肽 RN3 的中位抑制浓度为 37 nM。随后,噬菌体RN3被用作捕获探针,SARS-CoV-2 SP特异性结合肽Pn(序列:WNLDLSQWLPPMGGGGSKKKC)作为检测探针,以及基于Au@Pd NP的过氧化物酶模拟纳米酶用于信号放大。建立了噬菌体 RN3/抗原/肽 Pn 夹心 ELISA,用于选择性检测 SARS-CoV-2 SP,线性范围为 5-1000 pg/mL,检测限为 2.93 pg/mL。该方法应用于13个随机临床样本的双盲检测,可以区分阳性和阴性样本。检测结果与金标准RT-PCR方法的结果非常一致。因此,本研究提出噬菌体展示双肽来构建可靠且廉价的 ELISA 来筛查 SARS-CoV-2 感染。类似的策略可以扩展到其他病原体的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
作为SARS-CoV-2刺突蛋白(SARS-CoV-2 SP)的亚基,受体结合域(RBD)可以特异性结合人血管紧张素转换酶2(ACE2),从而实现病毒感染宿主细胞。
设计与对象
该方法应用于13个随机临床样本的双盲检测,可以区分阳性和阴性样本。
核心方法
建立了噬菌体 RN3/抗原/肽 Pn 夹心 ELISA,用于选择性检测 SARS-CoV-2 SP,线性范围为 5-1000 pg/mL,检测限为 2.93 pg/mL。
主要结果
检测结果与金标准RT-PCR方法的结果非常一致。
解释与创新
类似的策略可以扩展到其他病原体的研究。
科研与公卫意义
因此,探索能够以良好亲和力与SARS-CoV-2 RBD保守表位结合的探针,从而识别各种SARS-CoV-2变异体,用于诊断、疫苗接种和开发新的检测方法具有极其重要的意义。
局限与证据强度
随后,噬菌体RN3被用作捕获探针,SARS-CoV-2 SP特异性结合肽Pn(序列:WNLDLSQWLPPMGGGGSKKKC)作为检测探针,以及基于Au@Pd NP的过氧化物酶模拟纳米酶用于信号放大。

Biopanning of Peptides Specific to the Conserved Epitope of the SARS-CoV-2 Receptor-Binding Domain and Nanozyme-Assisted Dual Peptide-Based Sandwich ELISA for Ultrasensitive Detection of the SARS-CoV-2 Antigen

Authors: Mingyang Wang, Haipeng Yu, Wanjian Liu, Jinlong Yuan, Zihan Bian, Yuhan Chen, Aihua Liu
Original Abstract
As the subunit of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein (SARS-CoV-2 SP), the receptor-binding domain (RBD) can specifically bind to human angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2), enabling viral infection of host cells. Therefore, it is of utmost significance to explore probes that can bind to the conserved epitope of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD with good affinity, so as to recognize various SARS-CoV-2 variants for diagnosis, vaccination, and the development of new detection methods. Herein, we biopanned peptide RN3 (sequence: YSIDWVFHHPML) by phage display, which can bind to the SARS-CoV-2 RBD with excellent affinity and selectivity. Molecular dynamics simulation and molecular docking verified that the His8 and Met11 residues in peptide RN3 are bound to the Asn487 and Tyr489 residues of the conserved epitope of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD subunit on the SARS-CoV-2 SP (His8-Asn487 and Met11-Tyr489, respectively) by hydrogen bonding. Alanine scanning confirmed that Met11 of peptide RN3 is the key amino acid for binding to the SARS-CoV-2 SP. The median inhibition concentration for peptide RN3 inhibiting the SARS-CoV-2 SP binding to ACE2 was 37 nM. Subsequently, phage RN3 was used as a capture probe, the SARS-CoV-2 SP-specific binding peptide Pn (sequence: WNLDLSQWLPPMGGGSKKKC) as a detection probe, and Au@Pd NP-based peroxidase-mimicking nanozyme for signal amplification. A phage RN3/antigen/peptide Pn sandwich ELISA was established for the selective detection of SARS-CoV-2 SP with a linear range of 5-1000 pg/mL and a limit of detection of 2.93 pg/mL. This method was applied to double-blind testing of 13 randomized clinical samples, which could distinguish between positive and negative samples. The detected results are well consistent with those of the gold-standard RT-PCR method. Thus, this study proposed phage-displayed dual peptides to construct a reliable and inexpensive ELISA for screening SARS-CoV-2 infection. A similar strategy can be extended to study other pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
As the subunit of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein (SARS-CoV-2 SP), the receptor-binding domain (RBD) can specifically bind to human angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2), enabling viral infection of host cells.
Design and population
This method was applied to double-blind testing of 13 randomized clinical samples, which could distinguish between positive and negative samples.
Core methods
A phage RN3/antigen/peptide Pn sandwich ELISA was established for the selective detection of SARS-CoV-2 SP with a linear range of 5-1000 pg/mL and a limit of detection of 2.93 pg/mL.

The detected results are well consistent with those of the gold-standard RT-PCR method.
Interpretation and novelty
A similar strategy can be extended to study other pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
Therefore, it is of utmost significance to explore probes that can bind to the conserved epitope of the SARS-CoV-2 RBD with good affinity, so as to recognize various SARS-CoV-2 variants for diagnosis, vaccination, and the development of new detection methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
Subsequently, phage RN3 was used as a capture probe, the SARS-CoV-2 SP-specific binding peptide Pn (sequence: WNLDLSQWLPPMGGGSKKKC) as a detection probe, and Au@Pd NP-based peroxidase-mimicking nanozyme for signal amplification.

SARS-CoV-2 Delta 变体刺突蛋白:结构、机制以及与叶下珠属植物抗病毒配体的对接相互作用

SARS-CoV-2 Delta Variant Spike Protein: Structure, Mechanism, and Docking Interaction with Antiviral Ligands from the Plants of the Phyllanthus Genus
作者： Ashokkumar Sibiya, Baskaralingam Vaseeharan, Palaniappan Ramasamy, Sundaresan Bhavaniramya
摘要中文翻译2019年12月,中国湖北省武汉市首次发现“严重急性呼吸系统综合症冠状病毒2”突然爆发,随后在全球传播。感染这种高度传染性病毒的个人会造成严重的公共卫生危机。由于缺乏针对 nCoV-2019 的特定疫苗和抗病毒药物,加上病毒基因组中出现突变,因此需要采取多方面的方法来设计和发现 COVID-19。全面的全球监管计划对于准确预测和预防病毒感染至关重要。SARS-CoV-2刺突(S)蛋白在病毒结合、融合和进入中发挥着至关重要的作用,从而影响抗体和疫苗的产生和进化。SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白的受体结合域 (RBD) 对人类血管紧张素转换酶 2 (ACE2) 受体表现出显着的亲和力。它包含 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白 N 末端结构域 (NTD) 和受体结合结构域 (RBD) 的多种突变,从而增强免疫功效。B.1.617.2 突变体(也称为 Delta 变体)预计比早期版本传播得更快。因此,在本研究中,我们评估了 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白和人类 ACE2 蛋白的结构特征及其相互作用,并检查了源自叶下珠属植物化合物的假定 SPIKE-ACE2 蛋白-蛋白抑制剂。这是推进用于治疗病毒性疾病的单个植物成分的经济临床试验的关键方面。

SARS-CoV-2 Delta Variant Spike Protein: Structure, Mechanism, and Docking Interaction with Antiviral Ligands from the Plants of the Phyllanthus Genus

Authors: Ashokkumar Sibiya, Baskaralingam Vaseeharan, Palaniappan Ramasamy, Sundaresan Bhavaniramya
Original Abstract
An abrupt outbreak of "Severe Acute Respiratory Syndrome Coronavirus 2" was first identified in Wuhan City, Hubei Province, China, in December 2019, which was later disseminated globally. Individuals afflicted with this highly transmissible virus contribute to a significant public health crisis. The lack of specific vaccinations and antiviral medications for nCoV-2019, together with the emergence of mutations in the genome of the virus, necessitates a multifaceted approach to drug design and discovery for COVID-19. A comprehensive worldwide supervision plan is essential for the accurate forecast and prevention of viral infections. The SARS-CoV-2 spike (S) protein plays a crucial role in viral binding, fusion, and entrance, hence influencing the generation and evolution of antibodies and vaccines. The receptor binding domain (RBD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein exhibits a significant affinity for human angiotensin-converting enzyme 2 (ACE2) receptors. It encompasses diverse mutations in the N-terminal domain (NTD) and the receptor-binding domain (RBD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein, enhancing immunological efficacy. The B.1.617.2 mutant, also known as the Delta variation, is expected to spread more rapidly than earlier versions. Thus, in this study, we have evaluated the structural characteristics of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein and the human ACE2 protein and their interactions and examined putative SPIKE-ACE2 protein-protein inhibitors derived from the phytocompounds of Phyllanthus species. This is the crucial aspect for the advancement of economical clinical trials of individual plant components for the treatment of viral diseases.

COVID-19 大流行期间超额死亡率的差异取决于日本的婚姻状况。

Difference in excess mortality during the COVID-19 pandemic depending on marital status in Japan.
作者： Tasuku Okui
摘要中文翻译使用全国各地的数据对 2019 年冠状病毒病大流行期间日本不同婚姻状况的超额死亡率差异进行了调查。使用了 2010 年至 2023 年生命统计记录中的死亡率数据。年龄标准化死亡率是根据性别、婚姻状况、年份和死因计算的,并使用大流行前的值和准泊松回归模型来估计大流行后的预期死亡率。此外,大流行开始后,超额死亡人数相对于预期死亡人数(以下简称 P 值)的年龄标准化百分比是根据性别、婚姻状况和死因,使用预期和观察到的死亡人数来计算的。在大流行之前,无论性别和婚姻状况如何,所有群体的全因死亡率均呈下降趋势,而在大流行开始后,所有群体的死亡率均呈上升趋势。疫情前未婚的男性和女性全因死亡率下降幅度最大,而疫情后已婚人士的全因死亡率增幅最小。未婚男性和女性的全因死亡率年龄标准化 P 得分最高,而男性中已婚人士的全因死亡率最低。对于女性来说,各组的年龄标准化 P 分数更接近,婚姻状况类别之间的置信区间重叠。相比之下,年龄<65岁的女性中未婚者和已婚者的年龄标准化P值存在显着差异。总之,日本疫情期间未婚者的超额全因死亡率百分比最高,尤其是男性,未来继续监测这一趋势很重要。

问题与背景

设计与对象

主要结果
在大流行之前,无论性别和婚姻状况如何,所有群体的全因死亡率均呈下降趋势,而在大流行开始后,所有群体的死亡率均呈上升趋势。
解释与创新
总之,日本疫情期间未婚者的超额全因死亡率百分比最高,尤其是男性,未来继续监测这一趋势很重要。

局限与证据强度
对于女性来说,各组的年龄标准化 P 分数更接近,婚姻状况类别之间的置信区间重叠。

Difference in excess mortality during the COVID-19 pandemic depending on marital status in Japan.

Authors: Tasuku Okui
Original Abstract
Differences in excess mortality during the coronavirus disease 2019 pandemic across marital statuses in Japan were investigated using data from across the country. Mortality data from the Vital Statistics records spanning 2010-2023 were utilized. Age-standardized mortality rates were computed by sex, marital status, year, and cause of death, and expected post-pandemic rates were estimated using pre-pandemic values with a quasi-Poisson regression model. Furthermore, the age-standardized percentage of the excess number of deaths relative to the expected number of deaths (hereafter, P-score) after the pandemic started was calculated by sex, marital status, and cause of death, using the expected and observed number of deaths. A declining trend in all-cause mortality rate was observed across all groups before the pandemic, regardless of sex and marital status, while mortality rates increased across all groups after the pandemic began. The largest decrease in all-cause mortality rate was observed among never-married persons before the pandemic in both men and women, while the smallest increase after the pandemic was observed among married persons. The highest age-standardized P-score for all-cause mortality was observed among never-married men and women, while that for married persons was the lowest among men. For women, the age-standardizedP-scores were closer across groups, with confidence intervals overlapping among marital-status categories. In contrast, there was a significant difference in the age-standardized P-scores between never-married and married persons among women aged <65 years. In conclusion, the percentage of excess all-cause mortality was the highest among never-married persons during the pandemic period in Japan, particularly in men, and it is important to continue to monitor the trend in the future.

儿童 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险的免疫相关性:一项基于社区的前瞻性队列研究

Immune correlates of risk for SARS-CoV-2 infection in children: a prospective, community-based cohort study
作者： Katherine L. Hoffman, Grace Marshall, Collrane Frivold, Zack Acker, Isabel S. Arnould, Tara M Babu, Sarina R. Barnes, Melissa Briggs‐Hagen, Amanda M. Casto, Erica Clark
摘要中文翻译很少有研究描述儿童 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险的免疫相关性,特别是那些因疫苗接种和既往感染而具有混合免疫力的儿童。我们对 1509 名美国儿童(2022-2024 年)进行了一项基于社区的前瞻性队列研究,每周进行 SARS-CoV-2 PCR 检测,并测量针对多种变体的基线结合和中和抗体滴度。在调整年龄、近期感染、暴露环境和时间趋势后,较高的抗体水平,尤其是核衣壳结合抗体和 Omicron 特异性中和抗体,与 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险降低显着相关(调整后的风险比范围为每 log10 倍抗体水平 (AU/mL) 阳性单位差异 0.60 至 0.87)。二次分析表明,这些发现对于 SARS-CoV-2 免疫状态的多个分层来说是稳健的,与不同儿科年龄组相关,并且似乎适用于总体和症状感染风险。总之,结果表明特异性抗体可以预测具有不同免疫史的儿科人群的相对感染风险。随着 SARS-CoV-2 的不断发展,了解这些免疫相关性可能会为量身定制的疫苗接种策略和风险评估提供信息。了解儿童 SARS-CoV-2 感染风险的免疫相关性,尤其是具有混合免疫的儿童,仍有待探索。在这里,作者进行了一项队列研究,结果表明较高的核衣壳结合抗体和 Omicron 特异性中和抗体可显着降低感染风险,这表明这些标志物可以指导儿科人群的定制疫苗接种策略和风险评估。

Immune correlates of risk for SARS-CoV-2 infection in children: a prospective, community-based cohort study

Authors: Katherine L. Hoffman, Grace Marshall, Collrane Frivold, Zack Acker, Isabel S. Arnould, Tara M Babu, Sarina R. Barnes, Melissa Briggs‐Hagen, Amanda M. Casto, Erica Clark
Original Abstract
Few studies have characterized immune correlates of SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in children, particularly those with hybrid immunity from vaccination and prior infection. We conduct a prospective community-based cohort study of 1509 U.S. children (2022–2024), performing weekly SARS-CoV-2 PCR testing and measuring baseline binding and neutralizing antibody titers against multiple variants. Higher antibody levels, notably nucleocapsid-binding and Omicron-specific neutralizing antibodies, are significantly associated with reduced risk of SARS-CoV-2 infection after adjusting for age, recent infection, exposure settings, and temporal trends (adjusted hazard ratios ranging from 0.60 to 0.87 per positive unit difference in log10-fold antibody level (AU/mL)). Secondary analyses suggest these findings are robust to multiple stratifications of SARS-CoV-2 immune status, are relevant across different pediatric age groups, and appear to apply to both overall and symptomatic infection risk. Together, the results suggest that specific antibodies can predict relative infection risk in pediatric populations with diverse immune histories. Understanding these immune correlates may inform tailored vaccination strategies and risk assessments as SARS-CoV-2 continues to evolve. Understanding immune correlates of SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in children, especially those with hybrid immunity, remains underexplored. Here, the authors conduct a cohort study revealing that higher nucleocapsid-binding and Omicron-specific neutralizing antibodies significantly reduce infection risk, suggesting these markers could guide tailored vaccination strategies and risk assessments in pediatric populations.

Core methods

Interpretation and novelty

肺炎支原体诊断预测评分:是否可以区分肺炎支原体肺炎和 SARS-CoV-2 肺炎?

Mycoplasma pneumonia diagnostic prediction score: Is it possible to differentiate between Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia and SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia?
作者： Naoyuki Miyashita, Yasushi Nakamori, Makoto Ogata, Naoki Fukuda, Akihisa Yamura, Tomoki Ito
摘要中文翻译简介:肺炎指南推荐的肺炎支原体诊断预测评分是区分肺炎支原体肺炎和细菌性肺炎的有用方法。另一方面,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)是社区获得性肺炎中的常见微生物,因此我们研究了支原体评分是否可以区分肺炎支原体肺炎和SARS-CoV-2肺炎。方法:对 162 名肺炎支原体患者、422 名 Ancestral 株患者、262 名 Alpha 变种患者、274 名 Delta 变种患者和 1241 名 Omicron 变种患者进行了分析。结果:使用支原体评分预测肺炎支原体肺炎的敏感性为 71.8%。Ancestral 菌株的诊断特异性为 81.3%,Alpha 菌株的诊断特异性为 81.7%,Delta 菌株的诊断特异性为 77.4%,Omicron 菌株的诊断特异性为 88.2%。所有 SARS-CoV-2 肺炎病例的特异性为 84.8%。当针对当前流行的 Omicron 变体时,诊断特异性范围为 83.9% 至 92.7%,显示出亚变体之间的差异。使用四个参数来确定两组之间的差异:年龄、基础疾病、咳嗽的严重程度和快速诊断方法的使用。结论:比较肺炎支原体肺炎和 SARS-CoV-2 肺炎时,肺炎支原体诊断预测评分的诊断敏感性为 71.8%,诊断特异性为 84.8%。然而,当针对当前流行的 SARS-CoV-2 Omicron 变异肺炎时,诊断特异性提高到 88.2%,表明有可能区分肺炎支原体肺炎和 SARS-CoV-2 肺炎。但其特异性低于鉴别细菌性肺炎。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
另一方面,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)是社区获得性肺炎中的常见微生物,因此我们研究了支原体评分是否可以区分支原体。

Mycoplasma pneumonia diagnostic prediction score: Is it possible to differentiate between Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia and SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia?

Authors: Naoyuki Miyashita, Yasushi Nakamori, Makoto Ogata, Naoki Fukuda, Akihisa Yamura, Tomoki Ito
Original Abstract
INTRODUCTION: The Mycoplasma Pneumonia Diagnostic Prediction Score, recommended in pneumonia guidelines, is a useful method for differentiating Mycoplasma pneumoniae pneumonia from bacterial pneumonia. On the other hand, severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) is a common microorganism in community-acquired pneumonia, so we investigated whether the Mycoplasma Score can differentiate between M. pneumoniae pneumonia and SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia. METHODS: Analysis was performed on 162 patients with M. pneumoniae, 422 patients with the Ancestral strain, 262 with the Alpha variant, 274 with the Delta variant, and 1241 with the Omicron variant. RESULTS: When using the Mycoplasma Score, the sensitivity for predicting M. pneumoniae pneumonia was 71.8%. The diagnostic specificity was 81.3% for the Ancestral strain, 81.7% for the Alpha variant, 77.4% for the Delta variant, and 88.2% for the Omicron variant. The specificity for all SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia cases was 84.8%. When targeting the currently circulating Omicron variant, the diagnostic specificity ranged from 83.9% to 92.7%, showing differences among subvariants. Differences between the two groups were identified using four parameters: age, underlying disease, severity of cough, and use of rapid diagnostic methods. CONCLUSIONS: When comparing M. pneumoniae pneumonia and SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia, the diagnostic sensitivity of the Mycoplasma Pneumonia Diagnostic Prediction Score was 71.8%, and the diagnostic specificity was 84.8%. However, when targeting the currently circulating SARS-CoV-2 Omicron variant pneumonia, the diagnostic specificity increased to 88.2%, suggesting that it is possible to differentiate between M. pneumoniae pneumonia and SARS-CoV-2 pneumonia. However, the specificity is lower than that for differentiating bacterial pneumonia.

人工智能和机器学习在预测病毒学中的作用:预测、跟踪和对抗病毒威胁。

The Role of Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning in Predictive Virology: Forecasting, Tracking, and Combating Viral Threats.
作者： Mohamed A Farrag
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 危机充分说明了病毒大流行的威胁不断升级,暴露了传统反应性病毒学在应对快速演变的病原体方面的严重缺陷。本综述介绍了预测病毒学(PV)作为更广泛的流行病情报和公共卫生监测中人工智能(AI)驱动的学科,它使用先进的计算工具来预测病毒威胁并加速对策设计。当前的综述系统地研究了人工智能驱动的方法(例如机器学习和深度学习)如何通过整合大量基因组数据集、多模式监测信号和先进的计算模型来重塑病毒学,以在广泛传播发生之前预测病毒的出现和进化。讨论的 PV 核心支柱包括使用大型蛋白质语言模型的零次突变适应度和抗原逃逸预测;融合废水监测、数字流行病学、移动数据和社交媒体的多模式预警系统;基于神经微分方程的传输建模;用于广谱抗病毒药物和疫苗从头设计的生成式人工智能;人畜共患病溢出效应的生态风险评估。在针对深度突变扫描实验和现实世界流行病学结果(SARS-CoV-2变种、流感和其他疫情)的回顾性基准中,一些人工智能驱动的工具已证明其性能可与或超过传统方法,尽管大规模的前瞻性验证仍然有限。尽管取得了显着的进展,但仍然存在重大挑战,包括数据偏差、过度拟合历史模式、缺乏前瞻性验证以及跨环境的普遍性有限。此外,人们还担心机械可解释性、公平的全球数据集成和负责任的部署。本次审查还批判性地讨论了在全球范围内部署预测能力的道德、治理和公平影响。通过将尖端人工智能方法与病毒学见解相结合,并承认当前的局限性,这项工作提供了一个全面的框架,将病毒学从反应性学科转变为真正的预测性学科,最终加强全球卫生安全和大流行病防范。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述探讨了人工智能和机器学习在预测病毒学中的作用,重点关注预测、跟踪和对抗病毒威胁。它探讨了人工智能驱动的方法如何通过整合基因组数据集、监测信号和计算模型来重塑病毒学,以在广泛传播发生之前预测病毒的出现和进化。核心问题是人工智能如何将病毒学从反应性学科转变为预测性学科。
证据基础与方法
该评论系统地研究了机器学习和深度学习等人工智能驱动的方法。它集成了大量的基因组数据集、多模式监测信号和先进的计算模型。该审查批判性地解决了在全球范围内部署预测能力的道德、治理和公平影响。
共识与结论
在针对深度突变扫描实验和现实世界流行病学结果的回顾性基准测试中,多种人工智能工具的性能与传统方法相当或超过传统方法。预测病毒学的核心支柱包括零次突变适应度和抗原逃逸预测、多模式预警系统、基于神经微分方程的传播模型、用于抗病毒和疫苗设计的生成人工智能以及人畜共患病溢出的生态风险评估。
争议与缺口
大规模的前瞻性验证仍然有限。仍然存在重大挑战,包括数据偏差、过度拟合历史模式、缺乏前瞻性验证以及跨环境的普遍性有限。还存在对机械解释性、公平的全球数据集成和负责任的部署的担忧。
科研与实践启示
优先事项包括开发强大的前瞻性验证框架、解决数据偏差和过度拟合、提高机械可解释性、确保公平的全球数据集成以及建立负责任的部署协议。这项工作为将病毒学从反应性学科转变为预测性学科、加强全球卫生安全和大流行病防范提供了一个全面的框架。

The Role of Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning in Predictive Virology: Forecasting, Tracking, and Combating Viral Threats.

Authors: Mohamed A Farrag
Original Abstract
The escalating threat of viral pandemics, dramatically illustrated by the COVID-19 crisis, has exposed the critical shortcomings of conventional reactive virology in addressing rapidly evolving pathogens. This review introduces predictive virology (PV) as an artificial intelligence (AI)-driven discipline within broader epidemic intelligence and public health surveillance that uses advanced computational tools to forecast viral threats and accelerate countermeasure design. The current review systematically examines how AI-driven approaches ( e.g. , machine learning and deep learning) are reshaping virology by integrating vast genomic datasets, multimodal surveillance signals, and advanced computational models to anticipate viral emergence and evolution before widespread transmission occurs. Core pillars of PV discussed include zero-shot mutational fitness and antigenic escape prediction using large protein language models; multimodal early-warning systems that fuse wastewater monitoring, digital epidemiology, mobility data, and social media; neural differential equation-based transmission modeling; generative AI for de novo design of broad-spectrum antivirals and vaccines; and ecological risk assessment of zoonotic spillovers. In retrospective benchmarks against deep mutational scanning experiments and real-world epidemiological outcomes (SARS-CoV-2 variants, influenza, and other outbreaks), several AI-powered tools have demonstrated performance comparable to or exceeding traditional methods, although prospective validation at scale remains limited. Despite remarkable progress, significant challenges persist, including data bias, overfitting to historical patterns, lack of prospective validation, and limited generalizability across settings. In addition, there are concerns about mechanistic interpretability, equitable global data integration, and responsible deployment. This review also critically addresses the ethical, governance, and equity implications of deploying predictive capabilities at a global scale. By consolidating cutting-edge AI methodologies with virological insights and acknowledging current limitations, this work provides a comprehensive framework for transitioning virology from a reactive to a truly predictive discipline, ultimately strengthening global health security and pandemic preparedness.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines the role of artificial intelligence and machine learning in predictive virology, focusing on forecasting, tracking, and combating viral threats. It explores how AI-driven approaches reshape virology by integrating genomic datasets, surveillance signals, and computational models to anticipate viral emergence and evolution before widespread transmission occurs. The central question is how AI can transform virology from a reactive to a predictive discipline.
Evidence base and method
The review systematically examines AI-driven approaches such as machine learning and deep learning. It integrates vast genomic datasets, multimodal surveillance signals, and advanced computational models. The review critically addresses ethical, governance, and equity implications of deploying predictive capabilities at a global scale.
Consensus and conclusions
Several AI-powered tools have demonstrated performance comparable to or exceeding traditional methods in retrospective benchmarks against deep mutational scanning experiments and real-world epidemiological outcomes. Core pillars of predictive virology include zero-shot mutational fitness and antigenic escape prediction, multimodal early-warning systems, neural differential equation-based transmission modeling, generative AI for antiviral and vaccine design, and ecological risk assessment of zoonotic spillovers.
Controversies and gaps
Prospective validation at scale remains limited. Significant challenges persist, including data bias, overfitting to historical patterns, lack of prospective validation, and limited generalizability across settings. Concerns also exist about mechanistic interpretability, equitable global data integration, and responsible deployment.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include developing robust prospective validation frameworks, addressing data bias and overfitting, improving mechanistic interpretability, ensuring equitable global data integration, and establishing responsible deployment protocols. The work provides a comprehensive framework for transitioning virology from a reactive to a predictive discipline, strengthening global health security and pandemic preparedness.

成本和安全认知是沙特阿拉伯 COVID-19 疫苗接种意愿的决定因素

Cost and Safety Perceptions as Determinants of COVID-19 Vaccination Intention in Saudi Arabia
作者： Nurah Alamro, Turki M. Alhakbani, Abdullah AlDhuwaihy, Abdulrahman O. Alomar
摘要中文翻译背景:新发传染病爆发期间公众对疫苗的接受程度受到对安全性、可及性和成本的看法的影响。在大流行的早期阶段了解这些决定因素对于制定疫苗接种政策和沟通策略非常重要。本研究探讨了在全国疫苗推出之前,疫苗成本和安全性认知在沙特阿拉伯成年人中塑造 COVID-19 疫苗接种意愿方面的作用。方法:对 2020 年 8 月至 9 月期间居住在沙特阿拉伯的成年人进行了一项横断面电子调查。参与者是通过社交媒体和电子通信渠道在线分发招募的。该调查问卷评估了有关 COVID-19 的社会人口特征、知识和看法,以及在反映疫苗成本以及科学安全性和有效性证据的可用性的五种假设情景下接受 COVID-19 疫苗接种的意愿。进行多变量逻辑回归分析以确定与疫苗接种意愿相关的因素。结果:共有 1293 名受访者完成了调查。当免费提供疫苗接种时,57.5% 的参与者表示他们可能会接种疫苗。随着假设疫苗价格的上涨,疫苗接种意愿下降,当成本超过 100 SAR 时,疫苗接种意愿下降至 38.7%。当在安全性和有效性证据尚未得到证实的情况下接种疫苗时,意愿最低。在多种情况下,年龄、性别、教育程度和之前接种季节性流感疫苗与 COVID-19 疫苗接种意愿相关。结论:在沙特阿拉伯大流行的早期阶段,疫苗的可负担性以及对科学安全性和有效性证据的信心是与 COVID-19 疫苗接种意愿相关的重要因素。在不向接受者产生直接费用的情况下提供疫苗接种,并透明地传达安全性和有效性证据,可能会在未来大规模免疫运动中支持公众信心和疫苗接受度。这些发现应被解释为大流行早期的基线,不应推断为当前推出后的态度。

证据基础与方法
2020 年 8 月至 9 月期间,对居住在沙特阿拉伯的成年人进行了一项横断面电子调查。
共识与结论
随着假设疫苗价格的上涨,疫苗接种意愿下降,当成本超过 100 SAR 时,疫苗接种意愿下降至 38.7%。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
在不向接受者产生直接费用的情况下提供疫苗接种,并透明地传达安全性和有效性证据,可能会在未来大规模免疫运动中支持公众信心和疫苗接受度。

Cost and Safety Perceptions as Determinants of COVID-19 Vaccination Intention in Saudi Arabia

Authors: Nurah Alamro, Turki M. Alhakbani, Abdullah AlDhuwaihy, Abdulrahman O. Alomar
Original Abstract
Background: Public acceptance of vaccines during emerging infectious disease outbreaks is influenced by perceptions of safety, accessibility, and cost. Understanding these determinants during the early phase of a pandemic is important for informing vaccination policy and communication strategies. This study examined the role of vaccine cost and safety perceptions in shaping COVID-19 vaccination intention among adults in Saudi Arabia prior to national vaccine rollout. Methods: A cross-sectional electronic survey was conducted among adults residing in Saudi Arabia between August and September 2020. Participants were recruited through online distribution using social media and electronic communication channels. The questionnaire assessed socio-demographic characteristics, knowledge and perceptions regarding COVID-19, and intention to receive COVID-19 vaccination under five hypothetical scenarios reflecting vaccine cost and the availability of scientific safety and efficacy evidence. Multivariable logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with vaccination intention. Results: A total of 1293 respondents completed the survey. When vaccination was offered free of charge, 57.5% of participants reported that they would likely receive the vaccine. Vaccination intention declined as the hypothetical vaccine price increased, reaching 38.7% when the cost exceeded 100 SAR. Intention was lowest when vaccination was presented under conditions in which safety and efficacy evidence had not yet been confirmed. Age, gender, educational attainment, and prior seasonal influenza vaccination were associated with COVID-19 vaccination intention across several scenarios. Conclusions: Perceived vaccine affordability and confidence in scientific safety and efficacy evidence were important factors associated with COVID-19 vaccination intention during the early phase of the pandemic in Saudi Arabia. Providing vaccination without direct cost to recipients and communicating safety and efficacy evidence transparently may support public confidence and vaccine acceptance during future large-scale immunization campaigns. These findings should be interpreted as an early-pandemic baseline and should not be extrapolated to current post-rollout attitudes.

Scope and question
Public acceptance of vaccines during emerging infectious disease outbreaks is influenced by perceptions of safety, accessibility, and cost.
Evidence base and method
A cross-sectional electronic survey was conducted among adults residing in Saudi Arabia between August and September 2020.
Consensus and conclusions
Vaccination intention declined as the hypothetical vaccine price increased, reaching 38.7% when the cost exceeded 100 SAR.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Providing vaccination without direct cost to recipients and communicating safety and efficacy evidence transparently may support public confidence and vaccine acceptance during future large-scale immunization campaigns.

蛋白质组学分析确定了与 COVID-19 感染后血液恶性肿瘤患者免疫失调相关的途径

Proteomic analysis identifies pathways related to immune dysregulation in patients with hematologic malignancies after COVID-19 infection
作者： Bo Tang, Xiaodi Yang, Wenmin Tian, Jialin Zhu, Wei Liu, Mingyi Di, Huihui Liu, Zeyin Liang, Yang Chen, Yujun Dong
摘要中文翻译摘要 由于潜在的免疫功能障碍和治疗相关的免疫抑制,血液恶性肿瘤 (HMs) 患者特别容易感染 2019 年冠状病毒病 (COVID-19)。然而,与该人群不同临床轨迹相关的蛋白质组学特征仍未得到充分表征。我们对 40 名患有 COVID-19 的 HM 患者和 15 名健康对照者进行了血清蛋白质组分析。与对照组相比,HM 患者在 COVID-19 急性期表现出免疫相关反应受损。不同结果组的急性期蛋白质组模式有所不同;然而,由于结果组与最初的COVID-19严重程度、ICU入院和全身性疾病密切相关,并且由于样本量有限而未进行多变量调整,因此这些模式应被解释为与严重程度和结果相关的概况,而不是独立的轨迹特异性标记。致命病例显示出免疫激活失调的证据,而后来被分类为长期感染新冠病毒的患者则表现出更广泛的免疫相关途径抑制。除了免疫改变之外,与血小板活化和心脏相关功能障碍相关的途径也与不良临床轨迹相关。酶联免疫吸附测定验证支持所选蛋白质与急性感染期间结果组的关联。这些发现提供了 HM 患者中 COVID-19 的蛋白质组学概述,并为未来的机制研究和更大规模的外部验证队列提供了基础。重要性 由于先前存在的免疫功能障碍,血液系统恶性肿瘤患者极易感染严重的 2019 冠状病毒病 (COVID-19)、急性死亡和长期感染新冠病毒。然而,与不良临床轨迹相关的蛋白质组学特征仍然知之甚少。我们的血清蛋白质组学研究确定了与不同结果相关的不同急性期免疫特征:广泛的免疫抑制是长期新冠肺炎的特征,而免疫激活失调则与致命病例相关。血小板活化和心脏相关通路也与不良结果有关。这些发现为这一高危人群提供了关键的分子见解,支持未来生物标志物的开发、风险分层和有针对性的临床管理。临床试验 本研究在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册为 NCT05683353。该研究在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册为 NCT05683353。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究调查了感染 COVID-19 的血液恶性肿瘤 (HM) 患者的血清蛋白质组学特征,旨在表征免疫失调并确定与不同临床轨迹相关的途径。
证据基础与方法
该研究是一项蛋白质组学分析,涉及 40 名患有 COVID-19 的 HM 患者和 15 名健康对照者,其结果得到了酶联免疫吸附测定验证的支持。
共识与结论
与对照组相比,HM 患者在 COVID-19 急性期表现出免疫相关反应受损。致命病例显示出免疫激活失调的证据,而长期感染的新冠患者则表现出更广泛的免疫相关途径抑制。血小板激活和心脏相关功能障碍途径与不良临床轨迹相关。
争议与缺口
该研究承认,不同结果组的急性期蛋白质组学模式有所不同,但与最初的 COVID-19 严重程度、入住 ICU 和全身性疾病密切相关。由于样本量有限,未进行多变量调整,并且模式应解释为与严重性和结果相关的概况,而不是独立的轨迹特异性标记。
科研与实践启示
这些发现提供了 HM 患者中 COVID-19 的蛋白质组学概述,并为未来的机制研究和更大规模的外部验证队列提供了基础。它们支持未来针对这一高风险人群的生物标志物开发、风险分层和有针对性的临床管理。

Proteomic analysis identifies pathways related to immune dysregulation in patients with hematologic malignancies after COVID-19 infection

Authors: Bo Tang, Xiaodi Yang, Wenmin Tian, Jialin Zhu, Wei Liu, Mingyi Di, Huihui Liu, Zeyin Liang, Yang Chen, Yujun Dong
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Patients with hematologic malignancies (HMs) are particularly vulnerable to coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19) because of underlying immune dysfunction and treatment-related immunosuppression. However, proteomic features associated with different clinical trajectories in this population remain insufficiently characterized. We performed serum proteomic analysis in 40 HM patients with COVID-19 and 15 healthy controls. Compared with controls, HM patients showed impaired immune-related responses during the acute phase of COVID-19. Acute-phase proteomic patterns differed across outcome groups; however, because outcome groups were closely intertwined with initial COVID-19 severity, ICU admission, and systemic illness, and because multivariable adjustment was not performed due to the limited sample size, these patterns should be interpreted as severity- and outcome-associated profiles rather than independent trajectory-specific markers. Fatal cases showed evidence of dysregulated immune activation, whereas patients later classified as having long COVID exhibited broader suppression of immune-related pathways. In addition to immune alterations, pathways related to platelet activation and cardiac-related dysfunction were associated with adverse clinical trajectories. Enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay validation supported the association of selected proteins with outcome groups during acute infection. These findings provide a proteomic overview of COVID-19 in HM patients and offer a basis for future mechanistic studies and larger external validation cohorts. IMPORTANCE Patients with hematologic malignancies are highly vulnerable to severe coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), acute death, and long COVID due to preexisting immune dysfunction. However, the proteomic signatures linked to adverse clinical trajectories remain poorly understood. Our serum proteomic study identifies distinct acute-phase immune profiles associated with different outcomes: broad immune suppression characterizes long COVID, while dysregulated immune activation is associated with fatal cases. Platelet activation and cardiac-related pathways are also linked to poor outcomes. These findings provide key molecular insights for this high-risk population, supporting future biomarker development, risk stratification, and targeted clinical management. CLINICAL TRIALS This study is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov as NCT05683353. This study is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov as NCT05683353.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study investigates serum proteomic features in patients with hematologic malignancies (HMs) who contracted COVID-19, aiming to characterize immune dysregulation and identify pathways associated with different clinical trajectories.
Evidence base and method
The study is a proteomic analysis involving 40 HM patients with COVID-19 and 15 healthy controls, with findings supported by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay validation.
Consensus and conclusions
HM patients exhibited impaired immune-related responses during the acute phase of COVID-19 compared to controls. Fatal cases showed evidence of dysregulated immune activation, while long COVID patients exhibited broader suppression of immune-related pathways. Platelet activation and cardiac-related dysfunction pathways were associated with adverse clinical trajectories.
Controversies and gaps
The study acknowledges that acute-phase proteomic patterns differed across outcome groups but were closely intertwined with initial COVID-19 severity, ICU admission, and systemic illness. Due to limited sample size, multivariable adjustment was not performed, and patterns should be interpreted as severity- and outcome-associated profiles rather than independent trajectory-specific markers.
Research and practice implications
These findings provide a proteomic overview of COVID-19 in HM patients and offer a basis for future mechanistic studies and larger external validation cohorts. They support future biomarker development, risk stratification, and targeted clinical management for this high-risk population.

伊朗育龄妇女接种 COVID-19 疫苗后月经周期发生变化

Menstrual Cycle Changes among Reproduction-Aged Iranian Women Following COVID-19 Vaccination
作者： Afrooz Azad, Fatemeh Darsareh, Mahbobe Ebrahimi abshur, Mojgan Hajisafari, Azadeh Mahmoudi Essaabadi
摘要中文翻译背景:接种 COVID-19 疫苗后越来越多地报道月经周期紊乱,这引发了人们对其在育龄妇女中的患病率和临床意义的疑问。目的 本研究旨在调查伊朗育龄妇女接种不同剂量的 COVID-19 疫苗后月经周期改变的发生率和类型。方法 对已接种疫苗的女性进行横断面调查,报告每次疫苗接种前后的月经周期状况。收集并分析有关周期规律、血流特征和特定月经失调的数据。结果 在接种第一剂、第二剂、第三剂和第四剂疫苗后,分别有 28.8%、25.4%、30.3% 和 68.4% 的参与者报告月经周期发生变化。最常见的变化是第一剂和第二剂后月经稀发(8.9%和5.6%)、第三剂后月经过多(5.3%)和第四剂后月经量少(8.3%)。与国际研究的比较显示,患病率存在​​很大差异(从 25% 到 78%),这可能是由于方法、人群特征、疫苗类型和接种前健康状况的差异所致。结论 相当比例的伊朗女性在接种 COVID-19 疫苗后出现月经改变,最常见的是月经稀发、月经过多和月经量少。虽然这些发现通常具有自我限制性,但强调需要将月经健康纳入疫苗接种后监测和患者咨询中。未来的研究应该探索潜在的免疫内分泌机制以及这些变化的长期临床影响。

Menstrual Cycle Changes among Reproduction-Aged Iranian Women Following COVID-19 Vaccination

Authors: Afrooz Azad, Fatemeh Darsareh, Mahbobe Ebrahimi abshur, Mojgan Hajisafari, Azadeh Mahmoudi Essaabadi
Original Abstract
Background: Menstrual cycle disturbances have been increasingly reported after COVID-19 vaccination, raising questions about their prevalence and clinical significance among women of reproductive age. Objective This study aimed to investigate the incidence and types of menstrual cycle alterations following different doses of COVID-19 vaccines among Iranian women of reproductive age. Methods A cross-sectional survey was conducted among vaccinated women who reported their menstrual cycle status before and after each vaccine dose. Data on cycle regularity, flow characteristics, and specific menstrual disorders were collected and analyzed. Results Menstrual cycle alterations were reported by 28.8%, 25.4%, 30.3%, and 68.4% of participants after the first, second, third, and fourth vaccine doses, respectively. The most common changes were oligomenorrhea after the first and second doses (8.9% and 5.6%), menorrhagia after the third dose (5.3%), and hypomenorrhea after the fourth dose (8.3%). Comparisons with international studies revealed a wide variation in prevalence (ranging from 25% to 78%), which may be explained by differences in methodology, population characteristics, vaccine types, and pre-vaccination health status. Conclusion A considerable proportion of Iranian women experienced menstrual alterations following COVID-19 vaccination, most commonly oligomenorrhea, menorrhagia, and hypomenorrhea. While generally self-limiting, these findings highlight the need to integrate menstrual health into post-vaccination monitoring and patient counseling. Future research should explore the underlying immune–endocrine mechanisms and long-term clinical implications of these changes.

Evidence base and method
Methods A cross-sectional survey was conducted among vaccinated women who reported their menstrual cycle status before and after each vaccine dose.
Consensus and conclusions
Comparisons with international studies revealed a wide variation in prevalence (ranging from 25% to 78%), which may be explained by differences in methodology, population characteristics, vaccine types, and pre-vaccination health status.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
While generally self-limiting, these findings highlight the need to integrate menstrual health into post-vaccination monitoring and patient counseling.

SARS-CoV-2 引起的 COVID-19 患者氨基酸谱的系统评价

Systematic review of amino acid profiles among COVID-19 patients caused by SARS-CoV-2
作者： Saber Soltani, Hamid Choobineh, Fariba Nabatchian, Mohammad Kord, Bahram Nikmanesh, Farideh Razi, Houran Firouzian, Ziba Majidi
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 新出现的证据强调了氨基酸代谢在 COVID-19 发病机制和严重程度中的关键作用。该综述纳入的研究涵盖了疾病严重程度从轻度到危重的不同程度的患者。方法 按照 PRISMA 指南进行系统评价,并使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华工具评估研究质量,以探讨氨基酸谱与 COVID-19 患者临床结果之间的关系。对 PubMed、Scopus、Web of Science 和 Google Scholar 的全面搜索(从大流行开始到 2024 年 12 月的最新数据)发现了报告 COVID-19 患者氨基酸代谢原始数据的同行评审研究。结果 纳入了 14 项符合条件的研究,涉及 1355 名确诊的 COVID-19 患者(总共 1726 名参与者),结果显示氨基酸谱存在显着破坏。主要发现包括精氨酸、谷氨酰胺和色氨酸水平降低,同时苯丙氨酸和支链氨基酸 (BCAA) 水平升高。这些变化与疾病严重程度、免疫抑制、全身炎症和代谢重编程有关。尿素循环和犬尿氨酸途径等途径的失调与内皮功能障碍和免疫失调有关。结论 失调的氨基酸谱可能作为疾病严重程度的潜在生物标志物,在临床应用前需要进一步验证。恢复氨基酸平衡或调节代谢途径可以改善临床结果。需要进一步的研究来验证这些发现,并探索旨在减轻 SARS-CoV-2 感染的代谢后果的个性化治疗策略。

共识与结论
